Method: 针对三种信息结构（动作不对称、奖励不对称、二者兼有），将先前的Hoeffding型置信区间替换为基于KL散度的置信界，提出mKL-UCB、mKL-UCB-Intervals与mKL-DSEE三个算法。对奖励不对称情形，证明两条臂的KL区间在确定性样本数后分离；进一步分析M个独立玩家对消除过程的加速作用。

Result: 在每种信息结构下，基于KL散度的算法均给出严格更紧的遗憾保证。借助Pinsker不等式证明改进因子至少为2，当奖励均值接近0或1时改进幅度远大于2。对奖励不对称，证明两臂KL区间经过确定性样本数后分离；M个独立玩家可进一步加速消除。

Conclusion: 以KL散度取代Hoeffding型置信区间是多玩家多臂老虎机中更优的置信界设计，能显著改善遗憾性能并带来结构性理论保证。该框架适用于多种信息不对称情形，且玩家并行性可加速学习。

Abstract: We study the multiplayer multi-armed bandit problem with information asymmetry under Bernoulli rewards, for three information structures: asymmetry in actions, in rewards, and in both. Replacing the Hoeffding-style confidence intervals of prior work with Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergence-based bounds gives strictly tighter regret guarantees in each case. We propose \texttt{mKL-UCB}, \texttt{mKL-UCB-Intervals} and \texttt{mKL-DSEE}, and show that the improvement factor is at least two by Pinsker's inequality and far larger when reward means are near zero or one. For asymmetry in rewards we prove that two arms' KL intervals separate after a deterministic number of samples, and that $M$ independent players accelerate elimination further.

</details>


### [205] [A Hybrid AI Framework for Academic Advising: Integrating Ensemble-Based Grade Prediction and a Rule-Based Expert System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26243)
*Hamid Saadatfar,Rohollah Hedayati-Nasab,AmirHossein Eshghi,Arash Hajihashemi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文设计并实现了一个基于"聚类+堆叠集成"成绩预测与规则化推荐引擎混合框架的智能学业指导系统，在Birjand大学41万余条记录上取得RMSE 2.35的聚合预测效果，并已在校园门户落地应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生规模迅速增长给传统学术指导流程带来严峻挑战，人工指导难以满足实时、个性化的学业反馈需求，亟需智能化系统支撑学生学业进展。

Method: 采用两部分混合框架：第一步，基于Birjand大学416,558条教育记录，先用高斯混合模型（GMM）将学生划分为同质聚类，再为每个聚类单独训练由随机森林、梯度提升与MLP组成的Stacking集成模型进行成绩预测；第二步，构建规则化专家系统，将教育规章制度与预测成绩相结合，输出智能推荐。

Result: Stacking模型在所有聚类中均优于各基模型，最终聚合RMSE为2.35。系统已作为实用工具部署于Birjand大学门户，可为学生提供学期GPA预测、学业预警风险提示及提升GPA的选课建议等实时反馈。

Conclusion: 该研究表明"分群建模+堆叠集成"能有效提升成绩预测精度，并与规则化推荐引擎协同构成可落地的智能学业指导系统，为大规模学生群体的个性化指导提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The rapidly increasing student population has posed serious challenges to the traditional academic advising process. This study designs and implements a multi-purpose intelligent system to support students' academic progress, based on a two-part hybrid framework: (1) an advanced model for grade prediction and (2) a rule-based recommendation engine. Using a dataset containing 416,558 educational records from the University of Birjand, students were first divided into homogeneous clusters using the Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM). Subsequently, a Stacking Ensemble model combining Random Forest, Gradient Boosting, and MLP was trained specifically for each cluster. Evaluation results demonstrated that the Stacking model outperformed base models across all clusters, achieving a final aggregated RMSE of 2.35. The second component is an expert system that provides intelligent recommendations by synergizing educational regulations with the grades predicted by the first component. This system has been implemented as a practical tool on the University of Birjand portal, offering students real-time feedback such as semester GPA prediction, probation risk warnings, and course suggestions for GPA improvement.

</details>


### [206] [A Multi-Timestep LSTM Ensemble regressor for Enhanced Short-Term Runoff Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26244)
*Hamid Saadatfar,AmirHossein Eshghi,Behnaz Behdani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种基于LSTM并采用PSO优化的日径流预测模型，在伊朗Ajichay河上取得R²为74.95%–91.42%的良好精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确预测河流径流是水资源管理、洪水调控与农业规划的关键。Ajichay河作为伊朗西北部乌尔米耶湖的主要支流，近年面临日益加剧的水资源压力，亟需可靠的日尺度径流预测方法。

Method: 构建由5个LSTM单元组成的预测模型，每个单元分别在2至6天不同时间间隔上训练，以更好捕捉河流流量变化模式；并采用基于种群的粒子群优化（PSO）算法对每个模型进行超参数微调。以2017—2018年未见数据为测试集，采用R²、RMSE与MSE作为评价指标，同时应用多种特征重要性方法识别影响径流变化的主导变量。

Result: 模型表现出较强的预测精度，R²介于74.95%至91.42%之间；特征重要性分析进一步揭示了驱动径流变化的关键因子。

Conclusion: 多时间间隔LSTM与PSO优化相结合可有效提升日径流预测精度，为流域水资源管理与洪水防控提供支持。

Abstract: Accurately forecasting river runoff is key to managing water resources, controlling floods, and planning agriculture. This study examines the Ajichay River in northwest Iran, a major tributary of Lake Urmia that has experienced increasing water-related stress in recent years. We introduce a daily runoff prediction model based on Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks. The model combines five LSTM units, each trained on different time intervals ranging from 2 to 6 days, to better capture variations in river flow patterns. To improve performance, each model was fine-tuned using Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO), a population-based optimization algorithm. The proposed approach was evaluated on unseen data from 2017-2018 using $R^2$, RMSE, and MSE as performance metrics. The results showed strong predictive accuracy, with $R^2$ values ranging from 74.95% to 91.42%. In addition, multiple feature-importance methods were applied to identify the most influential variables, providing further insight into the factors that drive runoff variations.

</details>


### [207] [Coding Agents are Strong Prompt Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26261)
*Agamdeep Singh,Srishti Gautam,Priyanshu Gupta,Nikita Mehrotra,Tanmay Bakshi,Sumit Gulwani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 CASD（Coding-Agent Skill Distillation）：仅用静态轨迹语料，由通用编码代理编写并执行分析代码，做全语料统计反思，一次性离线合成优化提示，无需环境访问与验证数据，性能与成本均优于迭代式提示优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于搜索的提示优化器依赖"提议编辑— rollout 执行—评分—保留改进"的迭代闭环，需环境访问、验证数据与大量 rollout，成本高且流程冗余。作者质疑该优化循环的必要性，寻求以静态轨迹语料直接合成优化提示的更简方案。

Method: CASD 单次离线分析：给定静态 agent 轨迹语料，由现成编码代理编写并执行分析代码，计算全语料统计量、识别系统性失败模式、抽查代表性 episode，并将洞见蒸馏为行为规则写入优化提示。核心为"反思范围"的转变——从每步小批量轨迹推理转为全语料范围的统计反思，无需环境交互与验证数据。

Result: 在四个 agentic 基准（ALFWorld、τ²-bench retail 与 telecom、SpreadsheetBench-Verified）上，同等数据访问条件下单次 CASD 通过即在 3/4 基准上超过 SOTA 反思式优化器 GEPA，并在全部 4 个基准上超过验证门控反思搜索 SkillOpt；平均较未优化基线提升 16.6 个百分点（GEPA 10.9，SkillOpt 5.3）。单次优化成本约 $1.60，比验证门控搜索便宜 22 倍以上。即使对手获得额外验证数据与不受限环境访问，CASD 仍在 2/4 基准上领先。

Conclusion: 语料规模的统计反思可作为迭代搜索的有效替代方案用于提示优化：无需环境访问、验证数据与反复 rollout，单次离线分析即可获得更强且更廉价的提示，表明现有"迭代搜索"闭环并非必要。

Abstract: Search-based prompt optimizers improve prompts through iterative search: they propose edits, execute fresh rollouts, score the resulting trajectories, and retain only edits that improve a validation metric. We show that this optimization loop is unnecessary. Given only a static corpus of agent trajectories, an off-the-shelf coding agent can directly synthesize an optimized prompt, requiring neither environment access nor validation data. We call this approach \textit{Coding-Agent Skill Distillation} (CASD). The key insight is reflection scope. Rather than reasoning over a small batch of trajectories at each optimization step, the coding agent writes and executes analysis code to compute corpus-wide statistics, identifies systematic failure modes, inspects representative episodes, and distills the resulting insights into behavioral rules. Across four agentic benchmarks (ALFWorld, $τ^2$-bench retail and telecom, and SpreadsheetBench-Verified), under matched data access, a single CASD pass outperforms GEPA, a state-of-the-art reflective prompt optimizer, on three of four benchmarks and outperforms validation-gated reflective search (SkillOpt) on all four, improving the unoptimized baseline by 16.6 percentage points on average versus 10.9 for GEPA and 5.3 for SkillOpt. Because CASD performs a single offline analysis pass rather than iterative search, producing an optimized prompt costs approximately \$1.60---over $22\times$ cheaper than validation-gated search. Even when competing methods are granted additional validation data and unrestricted environment access, CASD remains ahead on two of four benchmarks. These results suggest that corpus-scale statistical reflection is a viable alternative to iterative search for prompt optimization.

</details>


### [208] [Decoupling Is Not Identification: Supervised Evidential Learning in Next-Token Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26268)
*Ge Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Evidential Next-Token Prediction (ENTOP) 框架，在字符级 Moby-Dick 上以精确 8-gram 计数作为可复现的词汇支持标签，审计 Dirichlet 头中浓度 S 是否真正编码训练支持。结果显示隐式证据训练几乎不携带计数信号，显式监督可泛化，但浓度即便通过识别性检验仍在校准与效用上失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一词概率只说明模型预测什么，不说明其背后有多少训练支持。Dirichlet 头虽能将均值 m 与浓度 S 解耦，但解耦本身并未识别 S 的语义。作者旨在填补这一缺口：检验浓度 S 是否真正对应可度量的训练支持，并建立可复现的审计方式。

Method: 在字符级 Moby-Dick 语料上构建 ENTOP 框架：以精确 8-gram 计数作为可复现的词汇支持标签，从 20% 上下文类型中扣留计数回归作为留出标签。比较隐式证据训练与显式监督；使用偏 Spearman 相关、匹配对胜率、AUROC 评估预测熵/监督 vacuity，并与索引化 CE 表示基线和语料 oracle 对比；进一步按最长后缀与表示距离分层，并调节 digamma 目标中的计数权重。

Result: 隐式证据训练在留出标签类型上除置信度外基本无计数信号（偏 ρ=0.001±0.014）；显式监督可泛化（ρ=0.201±0.010，匹配对胜率 0.822±0.021）。CE 预测熵对未见 8-gram 近乎随机（AUROC=0.490±0.004），监督 vacuity 达 0.772±0.003，接近索引化 CE 基线（0.769），但低于同义反复的语料 oracle（1.000）。最长后缀与表示距离分层均无法解释摊销成功之处。提高 digamma 目标中的计数权重仅以牺牲预测性能换取支持拟合。常数预测器在自然对数 RMSE 上胜出，vacuity 也未改善误差延迟。

Conclusion: 结果提出最小证据协议：置信度控制、匹配对、留出标签、常数基线与决策检验。研究表明浓度可通过识别性检验，却可能同时在校准与效用上失败，因此单靠解耦不足以赋予 S 可解释语义。

Abstract: A next-token probability says what a model predicts, not how much training support lies behind it. A Dirichlet head can represent this distinction by separating mean $m$ from concentration $S$, but decoupling does not identify what $S$ means. Here we propose an Evidential Next-Token Prediction (ENTOP) framework to audit this gap on character-level Moby-Dick, using exact 8-gram count as a reproducible lexical-support label and withholding count regression from 20% of context types. Standard implicit evidential training carries essentially no count signal beyond confidence on held-out-label types (partial Spearman $ρ= 0.001 \pm 0.014$), whereas explicit supervision generalizes ($ρ= 0.201 \pm 0.010$; matched-pair win $= 0.822 \pm 0.021$). CE predictive entropy is at chance for unseen 8-grams (AUROC $= 0.490 \pm 0.004$), while supervised vacuity reaches $0.772 \pm 0.003$, comparable with an indexed CE-representation baseline ($0.769$) but below the tautological corpus oracle ($1.000$). Neither longest-suffix nor representation-distance strata explain where amortization succeeds. Increasing count weight under the digamma objective improves support fit only by sacrificing prediction. A constant predictor wins natural log-RMSE, and vacuity does not improve error deferral. These results motivate a minimum evidence protocol---confidence control, matched pairs, held-out labels, a constant baseline, and a decision test---and show that concentration can pass identification while failing calibration and utility.

</details>


### [209] [FISSION: Label Augmentation for Bot Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26279)
*Sen Yang,Ignacy Nieweglowski,Aviv Yaish*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FISSION通过将每个账户的活动拆分为正样本子账户来生成标签，从而学习使机器人与协同操作账户彼此靠近的嵌入，并在维基百科傀儡账号和Twitter/X机器人检测上超越既有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人账户与协同影响操作多通过启发式方法发现，导致训练检测系统所需的可靠真实标签稀缺。

Method: 提出FISSION：将每个账户的活动切分为带正标签的子账户以生成标签源；基于此标签源训练检测模型，使其保留在正子账户间反复出现的行为规律性，并学习使机器人与同一协同操作账户相互靠近的嵌入。

Result: 在维基百科傀儡账号（sockpuppets）与Twitter/X机器人检测任务上，FISSION优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 利用活动拆分生成的正标签可在缺乏真实标签的情况下有效辅助表示学习与检测模型训练，提升协同操作与机器人检测性能。

Abstract: Bot accounts and coordinated influence operations are often discovered via heuristic methods, leaving a dearth of reliable ground-truth labels for training detection systems. To address this challenge, we study a natural question: can we generate labels to assist in learning embeddings in which bots and accounts from the same coordinated operation are close? We present FISSION, a method to generate labels by splitting each account's activity into positively labeled sub-accounts. Given this label source, we train detection models which preserve behavioral regularities recurring across positive sub-accounts. We evaluate FISSION and show it outperforms prior methods in detecting Wikipedia sockpuppets and Twitter/X bots.

</details>


### [210] [Dual-Frontier: When Can an Agent Trust Its World Model?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26293)
*Huatai Zhu,Qiang Chen,Ziqian Kou,Wenhao Li,Fei Wang,Yichao Cao,Xiu Su,Yi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将"决策失误究竟归因于决策规则还是世界模型"形式化为回报损失的反事实分解，证明被动交互下不可辨识，进而提出Dual-Frontier学习准则：仅当预测优势超出模型误差认证界时才允许决策，否则将证据分配给模型验证，并提供保证回报不降的值界与置信序列机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型世界模型通过预测动作后果支撑规划与决策，减少对高成本试错的依赖，但这也带来根本性歧义：当模型引导的决策失败时，仅凭轨迹无法判断是决策规则还是世界模型导致损失。该失败归因问题在现有工作中缺乏形式化刻画与可识别性分析，直接阻碍了对模型可信度的判定与证据的合理分配。

Method: 1) 将失败归因形式化为回报损失的反事实分解，并证明即使对有限时域规划器，其分量在被动交互下也不可辨识；2) 提出Dual-Frontier学习准则：仅当预测优势超过决策相关世界模型误差的认证界时接纳该决策，否则将证据分配至世界模型验证；3) 构建动作条件值界与闭环扩展以保障被接纳决策的回报非降；4) 采用校准门控与同时置信序列实现自适应证据复用，并给出充分且必要的验证界。

Result: 受控学习模型实验验证了所预测的失败模式与认证行为；跨骨干的工具使用基准在真实智能体世界模型流水线中实例化"先验证后提升"规则，一致地提升决策质量与可靠性。

Conclusion: 决策规则与世界模型误差的失败归因在被动数据下不可辨识，构成根本性障碍；Dual-Frontier通过认证界与证据分配将决策接纳与模型验证解耦，在理论与实验中同时保证回报非降并提升真实智能体流水线的决策质量与可靠性。

Abstract: Learned world models are becoming essential to general-purpose agents: by predicting action consequences, they support planning and decision-making while reducing reliance on costly trial and error. This reliance creates a fundamental ambiguity: when a world-model-guided decision fails, the trajectory alone may not reveal whether the agent's decision rule or the world model caused the loss. We formalize this failure-attribution problem as a counterfactual decomposition of return loss and prove that its components are not identifiable from passive interaction, even for finite-horizon planners. This obstruction motivates Dual-Frontier, a learning principle that admits a world-model-guided decision only when its predicted advantage exceeds a certified bound on decision-relevant world-model error; otherwise, evidence is allocated to world-model verification. Action-conditioned value bounds and a closed-loop extension guarantee non-decreasing return for admitted decisions. Calibrated gates and simultaneous confidence sequences support adaptive evidence reuse, with sufficient and necessary verification bounds. Controlled learned-model experiments validate the predicted failure modes and certification behavior, while cross-backbone tool-use benchmarks instantiate the same verify-then-promote rule in realistic agent world-model pipelines, consistently improving decision quality and reliability.

</details>


### [211] [Reliability Theory for AI Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26419)
*Grant Molnar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文将可靠性理论引入前沿AI控制，分析谷歌DeepMind防失控部署的防御栈，指出依失效域不同，罕见失效抑制可呈立方、平方或线性；Birnbaum重要度指示最高性价比改进，预防改变恢复需求群体，并给出分离、改进、测量与测试的具体建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠性理论已为分层系统提供成熟语言，但其形式化工具尚未成为前沿AI控制的标准做法；本文旨在把该理论应用于谷歌DeepMind的防失控部署防御，为AI控制系统提供更严格的分析框架。

Method: 采用可靠性理论的形式化工具，对同一控制栈在不同失效域下的罕见失效抑制进行建模；运用Birnbaum重要度评估组件改进对名义可靠性的边际贡献，并分析预防措施如何改变对恢复提出要求的群体。

Result: 同一控制栈的罕见失效抑制随失效域可表现为立方、平方或线性；Birnbaum重要度可识别哪些组件改进带来最大的名义可靠性收益；预防会改变恢复需求所作用的人群。

Conclusion: 可靠性理论可为前沿AI控制提供可操作指导，明确应分离、改进、测量和测试的对象。

Abstract: Reliability theory gives a mature language for layered systems, but its formal tools are not yet standard in frontier AI control. We apply them to Google DeepMind's defenses against rogue deployment. The same control stack can have cubic, quadratic, or linear rare-failure suppression depending on its failure domains. Birnbaum importance identifies which component improvements buy the most nominal reliability, while prevention changes the population on which recovery is demanded. These results give concrete guidance about what to separate, improve, measure, and test.

</details>


### [212] [The Source of Disturbance Matters: External, Internal, and Control-Generated Noise in Adaptive Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26428)
*Veronique Ziegler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在自适应代理的复制仿真中，持续的内部扰动导致最大的暴露与调控负担；当控制器正更新立即产生扰动代价时，有效扰动随代价升高呈非单调变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应调节本身可能扰动它试图稳定的状态。作者希望厘清不同扰动来源（外部扰动、持续内部扰动、控制生成扰动）及调控与扰动发生顺序如何影响暴露与控制器负担。

Method: 在复制仿真中设置一个自适应代理，比较三类扰动来源，并在“调控优先”与“扰动优先”两种时序下进行测试；同时让控制器正更新产生即时扰动代价，并在给定参数网格内扫描。

Result: 持续内部扰动在测试参数网格内产生最大的暴露与调控负担。当正更新带来即时扰动代价时，随代价增加出现非单调响应：有效扰动先上升，随机运行间变异性在中间区间增大，而在较高代价下纠正性活动被强烈抑制。

Conclusion: 扰动来源与时序共同塑造该模型中的暴露与控制器负担；后续研究应使用具有不同扰动来源的自适应代理，并将调控活动与暴露一并评估。

Abstract: Adaptive regulation can itself perturb the state it is intended to stabilize. In replicated simulations of an adaptive agent, we compare external disturbance, persistent internally generated disturbance, and control-generated disturbance under regulation-first and disturbance-first ordering. Persistent internal disturbance produces the largest exposure and regulatory burden within the tested parameter grid. When positive controller updates generate an immediate disturbance cost, increasing that cost produces a nonmonotonic response: effective disturbance initially rises, variability across stochastic runs increases over an intermediate range, and corrective activity becomes strongly suppressed at higher costs. The results show how disturbance source and timing shape exposure and controller burden in this model. They motivate testing adaptive agents with distinct disturbance sources and assessing regulatory activity alongside exposure.

</details>


### [213] [Reproducible AI Requires Reproducible Randomness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26461)
*Anthony Bertrand,Tom Schmitt,Engelbert Mephu Nguifo,David Hill*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究检验了"传递PRNG完整内部状态即可保证跨库输出一致"这一假设。对Mersenne Twister与Philox在Random、NumPy、PyTorch、TensorFlow四个Python生态中与参考算法比对，发现即使完整状态转移也无法保证可复现性，PyTorch的Philox甚至与参考算法根本不兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PRNG广泛用于蒙特卡洛模拟、随机计算与AI，其可复现性依赖相同内部状态下跨软件环境生成一致序列。常见假设是：只要转移生成器的完整内部状态（而非受限于整数的seed），不同库就应产生相同输出。本文旨在验证该假设是否成立，尤其是AI工作流常跨多框架，隐藏的实现差异可能破坏实验复制。

Method: 选取两个广泛部署的生成器Mersenne Twister和Philox，在Random、NumPy、PyTorch、TensorFlow四个主流Python生态中的实现，与原始参考算法在相同初始化条件下逐序列比对；并评估用户层面（不修改库源码）能在多大程度上恢复跨库可移植性与保真度。

Result: 可复现性不能仅由PRNG状态转移保证，即使实现声称遵循同一算法。部分实现达到保真，但另一些存在显著偏差；最突出的是PyTorch的Philox与参考算法存在根本性不兼容，无法跨环境精确复现输出。完整内部状态的转移不足以确保跨软件栈可复现。

Conclusion: PRNG的实现保真度是科学可复现性的必要条件；实现层面的设计选择会引入隐蔽的复制障碍，尤其在依赖多框架的AI流程中。论文贡献有二：一是给出在Python科学与AI生态中可靠使用PRNG、实现可复现的实践指南；二是评估了在不修改库源码的前提下，通过用户级技术恢复跨库可移植性与保真度的程度。

Abstract: Pseudorandom number generators (PRNGs) constitute indispensable computational tools across multiple scientific domains, including Monte Carlo simulations, stochastic computing, and artificial intelligence (AI). The reproducibility of such applications critically depends on the ability of PRNG implementations to generate identical sequences across software environments when initialized from the same internal state. These algorithms enable the simulation of stochastic processes while providing deterministic and repeatable behaviour, thereby facilitating reproducible experiments. Modern PRNG implementations may be initialized through either a seed or, more accurately, an initial state that exceeds the capacity of a conventional integer seed. However, reliance on a simple seed alone frequently proves insufficient to ensure consistent program execution traces across different implementations. A natural assumption is that transferring the complete internal state of a generator should guarantee identical outputs regardless of the software library used. This study examines the validity of this assumption by investigating whether complete initial states can ensure cross-library fidelity and portability of PRNG streams. We focus on two widely deployed generators, Mersenne Twister and Philox, and evaluate their implementations across four major Python ecosystems-Random, NumPy, PyTorch, and TensorFlow. We compare the sequences produced by these implementations against those generated by the original reference algorithms under identical initialization conditions. Our results demonstrate that reproducibility cannot be assumed from PRNG state transfer alone, even when implementations claim to follow the same underlying algorithm. While fidelity was successfully achieved for several implementations, significant discrepancies were observed in others. Most notably, the Philox implementation in PyTorch exhibits fundamental incompatibilities with the reference algorithm, preventing exact reproduction of generator outputs across environments. These findings challenge the common expectation that access to a full internal state of a PRNG is sufficient to ensure reproducibility across software stacks. They further highlight that implementation-specific design choices can introduce hidden barriers to experimental replication, particularly in AI workflows that rely on multiple frameworks. This work shows that implementation fidelity of a PRNG is a necessary condition for scientific reproducibility and makes two primary contributions. First, it identifies practical guidelines for achieving reliable PRNG usage and reproducibility within the Python scientific and AI ecosystem. Second, it evaluates the extent to which cross-library portability and fidelity can be recovered through user-level techniques, without requiring modifications to library source code.

</details>


### [214] [REFLEX with Jev for Efficient Selective Control in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26532)
*Tiantong Wu,Wei Yang Bryan Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: REFLEX 是一种用 Jev 作为快速类型化决策层、在低置信度或需生成时才调用强 LLM 的智能体架构，在固定基准上实现 95% 成功率并将强模型调用减少 72.7%，其可靠性受动作集规模与授权边界附近近有效替代项影响，优势在普通路由已高准确时受限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体常用生成模型处理有界决策，但并非所有决策都需强模型，研究何时可在不降低任务成功率的前提下更高效地处理这些决策。

Method: 提出 REFLEX 架构：以 Jev 作为快速类型化决策层，低置信度或需要生成时回退调用强 LLM；在 100 任务固定基准上评估，比较三类回退族，并通过受控干预与外部 BFCL、τ 风格评测检验。

Result: 在固定 100 任务基准上达到 95% 成功率，强模型调用减少 72.7%，且减少效果在三种回退族中持续；受控干预显示可靠性依赖动作集规模与授权边界附近的近有效替代项；外部评测显示在普通路由已高度准确时，相对廉价生成级联的优势有限。

Conclusion: 该研究界定了带 Jev 的选择性控制可降低计算的适用场景及其收益受限的条件。

Abstract: LLM agents often use generative models for bounded decisions, raising the question of when these decisions can be handled more efficiently without reducing task success. We study REFLEX, an agent architecture that uses Jev as a fast, typed decision layer and calls a strong LLM when confidence is low, or generation is required. On a frozen 100-task benchmark, REFLEX achieves 95% success with 72.7% fewer strong-model calls than a strong-only agent, with reductions persisting across three fallback families. Controlled interventions show that reliability depends on action-set size and near-valid alternatives near authorization boundaries. External BFCL and $τ$-style evaluations reveal limited advantages over a cheap generative cascade when ordinary routing is already highly accurate. These findings identify when selective control with Jev can reduce computation and where its benefits are limited.

</details>


### [215] [JEV-as-a-Judge: Accept When Confident, Escalate When Unsure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26550)
*Yubo Li,Yidi Miao,Ramayya Krishnan,Rema Padman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究"仅输出决策"的评判模型能否作为经济高效的初筛手段，并识别何时需要更强评判。JEV 在常规偏好与事实性任务上接近最强 LLM 评判器（差距≤3个百分点），但成本仅为其 0.36%；在需要校验推导或抵抗精心构造错误答案时差距较大，且差距集中于低置信度决策。采用置信度级联策略可保留 99% 的精度并降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-judge 虽可跨任务评估，但大规模应用时推理成本高、置信度可靠性存疑。作者希望探究一种仅输出决策（decision-only）的评判器能否承担低成本的初步筛选，并界定其在何种情形下失效、需要升级到更强的评判方式。

Method: 将 JEV-as-a-judge 与 16 个生成式及奖励模型评判器进行对比，并引入盲法人工裁决作为参照。分析中按任务类型（普通偏好、证据支撑的事实性、需要校验推导、需抵抗精心撰写的错误答案）分层比较精度差距与成本，并考察差距与置信度的关系；进而构建"冻结级联"（frozen cascade）：置信度高的判决直接采纳，置信度低的升级给强评判器。

Result: 在普通偏好与证据支撑的事实性任务上，JEV 与最强对比对象（SOTA LLM 评判器）的精度差距在 3 个百分点以内，而费用仅为该对比对象的 0.36%。当判决需要校验推导过程或抵抗精心撰写的错误答案时，差距显著扩大。在若干基准上，JEV 与该对比对象的差距主要集中在低置信度决策上。冻结级联在采纳高置信判决、升级不确定判决后，可保留该对比对象 99% 的精度且成本更低。

Conclusion: 仅输出决策的评判器可作为经济可行的首轮评估方案，在常规任务上逼近 SOTA 水平；其性能短板可预测地出现在高难度推理与对抗性错误答案场景，并可通过置信度级联加以缓解。置信度可作为有效的路由信号，实现精度与成本的折衷。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-judge enables evaluation across diverse tasks, but inference cost and confidence reliability become critical at scale. We study whether a decision-only judge can provide an economical first pass and identify when stronger evaluation is needed. Comparing jev-as-a-judge with sixteen generative and reward-model judges, with blinded human adjudication, we find it within three percentage points of a state-of-the-art LLM judge, our strongest comparator, on ordinary preference and evidence-grounded factuality at 0.36% of the comparator's fee. Larger gaps arise when judgments require checking a derivation or resisting an elaborately written wrong answer. On several benchmarks, JEV's gap to this comparator is concentrated in low-confidence decisions. A frozen cascade that accepts confident verdicts and escalates uncertain ones retains 99% of the comparator's accuracy at lower cost.

</details>


### [216] [Neutral-Atom-based Quantum Optimization for Resource Allocation in NOMA Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26556)
*Patatchona Keyela,Remon Polus,Soumaya Cherkaoui,Ola Ahmad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将NOMA上行网络中的最大接入问题（MAP）等价重构为图论中的最大独立集（MIS）问题，并利用基于里德堡原子阵列的中性原子量子计算平台求解，验证了该方案在大规模无线资源优化中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线通信网络中大量资源优化问题因组合特性与高计算复杂度而属于NP难问题。中性原子量子计算凭借量子叠加与纠缠，被视为高效求解此类问题的有前景平台，但其在无线通信优化中的应用尚鲜有探索。

Method: 将MAP建模为混合整数规划，联合考虑接入控制、用户分簇、信道分配与功率分配。为降低计算负担，将原问题等价重构为图论中的MIS问题，进而利用基于里德堡原子阵列的中性原子平台，将MIS自然地编码到量子系统的物理几何与阻塞（blockade）约束中。

Result: 数值结果表明，该中性原子量子方法在求解大规模无线资源优化问题方面具备可行性与潜力。

Conclusion: 中性原子量子平台结合MIS重构可有效应对NOMA上行网络中的MAP，为无线资源优化提供了一条新的量子计算求解路径。

Abstract: In wireless communication networks, many resource optimization problems are nondeterministic polynomial-time hard (NP-hard) due to their combinatorial nature and high computational complexity. Recently, neutral-atom-based quantum computing has emerged as a promising platform for efficiently solving such problems by leveraging quantum superposition and entanglement. However, its application to wireless communication optimization problems remains largely unexplored. In this paper, we investigate the use of neutral-atom quantum platforms to solve the maximum access problem (MAP), formulated as a mixed-integer programming task that jointly considers admission control, user clustering, channel assignment, and power allocation in a non-orthogonal multiple access (NOMA)-enabled uplink network. To reduce the computational burden, the MAP is equivalently reformulated as a maximum independent set (MIS) problem in graph theory. This reformulation enables the use of the neutral atom platform based on Rydberg atom arrays, where the MIS problem is naturally encoded into the physical geometry and blockade constraints of the quantum system. Numerical results demonstrate the feasibility and potential of this approach for addressing large-scale wireless resource optimization problems.

</details>


### [217] [Quantum-Aided Active Device Detection in Energy-Harvesting Symbiotic Radio Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26565)
*Remon Polus,Deemah Tashman,Soumaya Cherkaoui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种能量收集型码域NOMA共生无线电系统，IoT设备利用环境上行信号供能并通过LDS码反向散射，采用Grover量子搜索算法进行活跃设备检测，在接近最大似然性能的同时将oracle查询复杂度二次降低，适用于大规模环境IoT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代网络的大规模连接要求IoT部署具备高能量与频谱效率。共生无线电（SR）可使无源IoT设备借助反向散射现有蜂窝传输进行通信，但上行SR中的活跃设备检测（ADD）直接影响译码可靠性、干扰管理与系统吞吐，穷举最大似然检测复杂度过高，成为关键瓶颈。

Method: 构建能量收集型码域非正交多址接入共生无线电（NOMA-SR）系统：IoT设备从环境上行信号中收集能量，并使用低密度扩频（LDS）码反向散射信息；为降低ADD复杂度，引入Grover量子搜索算法替代穷举ML搜索。

Result: Grover量子搜索相较穷举ML搜索在oracle查询复杂度上实现二次降低；数值结果表明所提方法性能紧接近ML，同时大幅减少搜索迭代次数。

Conclusion: 所提能量收集码域NOMA-SR结合Grover量子搜索的ADD方案在保持接近最优检测性能的同时显著降低复杂度，展现出面向可扩展环境IoT系统的应用潜力。

Abstract: Massive connectivity in next-generation networks demands energy- and spectrum-efficient solutions for large-scale Internet of Things (IoT) deployments. Symbiotic radio (SR) enables passive IoT devices to communicate by backscattering existing cellular transmissions. A key challenge in uplink SR is active device detection (ADD), which directly affects decoding reliability, interference management, and system throughput. We propose an energy-harvesting code-domain non-orthogonal multiple access (NOMA)-SR system in which IoT devices harvest energy from ambient uplink signals and backscatter information using low-density spreading (LDS) codes. To reduce the complexity of ADD, Grover's quantum search algorithm is employed, providing a quadratic reduction in oracle-query complexity over exhaustive maximum-likelihood (ML) search. Numerical results show that the proposed approach closely approaches ML performance while substantially reducing the number of search iterations, demonstrating its potential for scalable ambient IoT systems.

</details>


### [218] [The Delegation Blind Spot: Auditing Product Decisions from Agent Choices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26642)
*Shivam Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出面向决策的审计工作流：将已声明的观察通道与产品价值对比映射为相容区间与见证总体，通过冻结实验、探索性跟进与受控仿真区分结构性歧义、弱识别与标定精度不足，并给出源标记决策回执与离线查看器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成功执行智能体任务并不需要识别用户未来会看重哪一项产品改进；现有评估多关注执行准确率，缺少针对具体决策是否可被数据识别、为何未解决的诊断框架。

Method: 基于既有识别理论与决策理论构建决策特定审计，输出相容区间与见证总体；采用冻结实验（两个固定模型快照、4,800 次请求、共享合成任务）、探索性跟进（2,400 次调用、记录所给偏好）、确定性抽取器（无需模型调用与标定观测），并以 14,400 次受控多项仿真分离结构性歧义、弱识别与有限标定精度。

Result: 36 个保守主区间在不同执行准确率下均未解决；跟进实验在每个模型 9 组比较中解决 3 组；确定性抽取器无需模型调用即解决 9 组中的 7 组，揭示模型生成报告引入的不必要不确定性；仿真进一步区分了未解决决策的成因。

Conclusion: 应保留决策相关的结构化输入，并在收集更多遥测数据前诊断决策未解决的原因；提出源标记决策回执与离线审计查看器。研究不含人类被试或真实客户结果，附完整证明、原始模型溯源、受控实验与可复现分析。

Abstract: Successful agent execution need not identify which future product improvement its user would value. We present a decision-specific audit that maps a declared observation channel and product-value contrast to compatible intervals and witness populations. Its foundations are established identification and decision theory; the contribution is an executable measurement workflow and a controlled study of its limits. A frozen experiment makes 4,800 requests to two pinned model snapshots on shared synthetic tasks. All 36 conservative primary intervals remain unresolved despite different execution accuracy. An exploratory 2,400-call follow-up records supplied preferences and resolves three of nine comparisons per model. A deterministic extractor resolves seven of nine without model calls or calibration observations, exposing unnecessary uncertainty introduced by model-generated reports. A further 14,400 controlled multinomial simulations distinguish structural ambiguity from weak identification and finite calibration precision. We propose a source-labeled decision receipt and provide an offline viewer for inspecting the audit. These results motivate preserving decision-relevant structured input and diagnosing why a decision is unresolved before collecting more telemetry. The study contains no human participants or real customer outcomes. Full proofs, raw model provenance, controlled experiments, and reproducible analyses accompany the report.

</details>


### [219] [Type-Safe Is Not Error-Free: A Constrained Decision Head Follows the Option Name, Not the Rubric Bound to It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26758)
*Yu Sun,Junhao Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文发现类型化决策模型虽保证输出符合schema（类型错误率恒为0%），但选项名称的语义极性会系统性逆转模型决策，揭示"格式合规"不等于"语义正确"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类型化（typed）决策模型面向软件直接消费，输出为预定义选项而非自由文本，从而在构造上保证输出符合schema。但这一保证无法回答模型是否按预期解释各选项含义。作者因此研究：改变选项名称与评分标准（rubric）的对应关系，是否会影响模型的决策—即"类型正确性"是否掩盖了语义层面的脆弱性。

Method: 对Jev及两个同类开放权重模型，在保持问题、状态、rubric措辞和选项名称集合完全不变的前提下，仅改变哪个选项名称被分配给哪个rubric。在1200个带任务专属rubric的工作流决策上，将两个选项从0/1重命名为no/yes；设置中性选项名称作为对照，覆盖4个谓词；并考察选项数量增加、读出几何（第二个模型族对完整选项跨度做均值池化）、托管模型以及将选项名替换为随机字符串等条件。

Result: 从0/1改为no/yes使每百个答案多改变70.4个（95% CI: [67.6, 73.1]），AUC由.94降至.23，显示决策排序发生系统性反转而非单纯不确定性；中性选项名下该操作几乎无影响。该模式在全部4个谓词上成立，效应至少为中性对照的7.4倍，并随选项数增加而增强。采用均值池化的第二模型族翻转频率低4.1倍。托管模型AUC由.8146变为.5806，答案翻转数是其重测底线的24倍。相反，用随机字符串替换选项名可使所有模型族回到中性对照状态，且不降低准确率。所有条件下类型错误率恒为0%。

Conclusion: 该失效取决于选项名称的语义极性，而非重命名操作本身。类型化输出的schema保证不能确保模型按预期语义解释选项；即使在决策准确率大幅下降时，类型错误率仍为0%，说明类型合规与语义正确性相互独立。

Abstract: Typed decision models are built for settings where model outputs are consumed directly by software. Instead of generating free-form text, they return a decision over a predefined set of options. By construction, every output conforms to the required schema. Yet this guarantee does not tell us whether the model interprets the options as intended. We study Jev and two Jev-like models with open weights by changing how option names are assigned to rubrics. Each option consists of an option name and a textual rubric that defines what the option means. We change only which option name is assigned to each rubric; the question, state, rubric wording, and set of option names remain exactly the same. On 1200 workflow decisions with task-specific rubrics, renaming the two options from 0/1 to no/yes changes 70.4 more answers per hundred (95% CI: [67.6, 73.1]) and shifts AUC from .94 to .23, revealing a systematic reversal in the decision ranking rather than simple uncertainty. The same operation has little effect with neutral option names. This pattern holds across all 4 predicates, where the effect is at least 7.4x larger than under the neutral control, and becomes stronger as the number of options increases. The effect also depends on the read-out geometry: a second model family that mean-pools over the full option span flips 4.1x less often. The hosted model exhibits the same behavior: the swap changes AUC from .8146 to .5806 and produces 24x as many answer flips as its test-retest floor. In contrast, replacing the option names with random character strings returns all model families to the neutral-control regime without reducing accuracy. The failure therefore depends on the semantic polarity of the option names rather than on the renaming operation itself. Across all conditions, the type-error rate remains 0%, even when decision accuracy degrades substantially.

</details>


### [220] [Are Human-Aligned Models Models of Humans? A Turing-Test Gap in Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23640)
*Suqin Yuan,Runqi Lin,Muyang Li,Guanzhe Hong,Jindong Gu,Lei Feng,Chris Russell,Tongliang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文区分了"对齐人类偏好"与"对齐人类行为"，提出"图灵测试差距"概念，证明偏好对齐在一般条件下会使模型行为更不像人类，实证显示人类响应似然损失随偏好加权强度增加而增大，且标准DPO中同样存在该差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍将基于人类反馈的对齐等同于"与人类对齐"，但人们希望AI给出的回复未必是自身会给出的回复。作者希望厘清"偏好对齐"与"行为对齐"的差异，并质疑"偏好对齐即提升人类相似性"这一隐含假设。

Method: 理论上：给出偏好对齐保持人类响应分布的充分条件，并检验真实人类偏好是否满足该条件。实证上：考察偏好加权强度（无论方向）对人类响应似然的影响，并测试标准DPO下的表现。

Result: 偏好对齐仅在一种严格条件下才能保持人类响应分布，且无一致证据表明真实人类偏好满足该条件；人类响应似然损失随偏好加权强度增大而增大，与加权方向无关；标准DPO中也出现同样的差距。

Conclusion: 人类相似性应被视为对齐的一个显式独立维度，而非由偏好对齐自动带来的副产品；偏好对齐可能以牺牲人类相似性为代价。

Abstract: Human-feedback alignment has made language models useful assistants and is commonly described as aligning them with humans. However, the responses people prefer from an AI need not be the responses they themselves would give. We distinguish alignment with human preferences from alignment with human behavior, and show that alignment with human preferences can make model behavior less human-like even when both preferences and responses come entirely from humans. We call this the Turing-test gap. We show that preference alignment preserves the human response distribution only under a restrictive condition, and find no consistent evidence that real human preferences satisfy it. Empirically, the loss of human-response likelihood increases with the strength of preference weighting, regardless of its direction, and the gap also appears under standard DPO. These results establish human-likeness as an explicit dimension of alignment rather than something assumed to follow from preference alignment.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [221] [TRACTOR Benchmark for Evaluating C to Rust Translators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25121)
*Hamed Okhravi,Brandt Ogden,Noah Luther,Ian McQuoid,Howard Mak,Nathan Burow*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该报告介绍了为DARPA TRACTOR项目开发的标准化C-to-Rust翻译基准及评估基础设施，用于系统评估翻译工具的正确性、安全性、地道性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: C/C++等内存不安全语言编写的关键软件长期存在内存安全漏洞。编程语言、程序分析和人工智能的进步为通过自动翻译到Rust等内存安全语言实现遗留系统现代化提供了新机遇，但缺乏系统性评估C-to-Rust翻译工具手段。

Method: MIT Lincoln Laboratory作为DARPA TRACTOR项目的独立测试与评估机构，开发了标准化基准，包含难度递进的测试集与更大规模的里程碑项目，并配套评估基础设施与度量指标。

Result: 构建了涵盖正确性、安全性、地道性（idiomaticity）和性能的评估指标体系及基准测试，基准与评估基础设施已公开可用。

Conclusion: 该基准和评估基础设施支持C-to-Rust翻译技术的广泛开发与评估，助力遗留系统向内存安全语言规模化迁移。

Abstract: Memory-safety vulnerabilities remain a persistent source of security risk in critical software, much of which is implemented in memory-unsafe languages such as C and C++. Recent advances in programming languages, program analysis, and artificial intelligence have created new opportunities to modernize these legacy systems through automated translation to memory-safe languages such as Rust. The DARPA Translating All C to Rust (TRACTOR) program seeks to develop scalable techniques for translating large C codebases into safe, performant, and maintainable Rust. MIT Lincoln Laboratory serves as the program's independent test and evaluation organization and has developed a standardized benchmark for systematically assessing C-to-Rust translation tools. This report describes the TRACTOR benchmark, including progressively challenging test batteries and larger milestone projects, as well as the supporting evaluation infrastructure and metrics for assessing correctness, safety, idiomaticity, and performance. The benchmark and associated evaluation infrastructure are publicly available to support the broader development and evaluation of C-to-Rust translation technologies.

</details>


### [222] [TAILOR: Template-Preserving Augmentation for Long-Tailed Log Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25261)
*Sepideh Hodaeian,Zhenhao Li,An Ran Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究日志解析中长期被忽视的稀有日志组（少于5条实例）问题，并提出通过模板保持增强来提升稀有组解析准确率的框架TAILOR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实日志数据集呈现高度不平衡的长尾分布：少数频繁模板主导评测指标，而稀有但运维重要的模板性能被掩盖，导致评测结果过于乐观。作者发现Loghub-2.0中稀有日志组占近20%模板但不足0.01%日志消息，现有解析器在这些组上性能显著下降。

Method: 提出TAILOR日志解析框架：在模板推断前，对稀有日志组进行模板保持增强（template-preserving augmentation），即用与模板一致的日志消息丰富稀有组，为区分静态词元与动态变量提供额外结构证据。该策略可跨不同LLM骨干泛化，无需修改解析器核心架构。

Result: TAILOR在稀有日志组上的解析准确率较最强基线提升19%，同时在完整数据集上保持有竞争力的性能；该增强策略在不同LLM骨干及现有LLM解析器上均能一致地带来提升。

Conclusion: 稀有日志组是日志解析评测中被系统性忽视但影响显著的问题，通过模板保持的数据增强可在不改变模型架构的前提下有效缓解长尾失衡带来的性能退化。

Abstract: Log parsing is essential for system log analysis because it supports tasks such as debugging, monitoring, and anomaly detection by transforming unstructured log messages into structured log templates. However, real-world log datasets exhibit highly imbalanced, long-tailed distributions, where a small number of frequent templates dominate while many rare templates appear only a few times. This imbalance causes evaluation results to be overly optimistic by allowing frequent templates to dominate benchmark metrics, while poor performance on rare yet operationally important events remains largely hidden. In this paper, we investigate the prevalence and impact of rare log groups, defined as log groups with fewer than five instances. Our empirical study on the widely used Loghub-2.0 benchmark shows that rare log groups account for nearly 20% of all templates but less than 0.01% of log messages. Because they contain only a handful of instances, all evaluated parsers experience substantial performance degradation on these groups. To address this challenge, we propose TAILOR, a log parsing framework that improves template inference for rare log groups through template-preserving augmentation. TAILOR enriches rare log groups with template- consistent log messages before template inference. The additional structural evidence helps distinguish static tokens from dynamic variables. Our experimental results show that TAILOR improves parsing accuracy on rare log groups by 19% over the strongest baseline while maintaining competitive performance on complete datasets. We further show that the proposed augmentation strategy generalizes across different LLM backbones and consistently improves existing LLM-based parsers without modifying their core architectures.

</details>


### [223] [The Vocabulary of Flaky Tests in Swift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25516)
*João Medeiros,Denini Silva,Breno Miranda*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文首次评估基于词汇的机器学习方法在Swift项目中的flaky test预测效果，采集15个开源项目数据训练五种分类器，随机森林取得最优（F1=0.86，AUC=0.95），并揭示了flakiness与稳定性两类词汇信号及其预测边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flaky test导致非确定性结果而代码未变，会削弱CI信心并延误交付。基于词汇的机器学习预测在Java和JavaScript中已被证明有效，但尚无研究针对Swift语言进行评估，而Swift的测试风格以UI和异步代码为主，可能存在不同的flakiness模式。

Method: 通过重复执行和提交历史挖掘，从15个开源Swift项目中采集91个flaky测试和22,349个稳定测试；基于TF-IDF unigram+bigram特征，在分层5折交叉验证下训练五种分类器（随机森林、决策树、朴素贝叶斯、SVM、KNN），并进行信息增益分析与错误分析。

Result: 随机森林表现最佳（Precision=0.92，F1=0.86，AUC=0.95），显著优于平凡基线（MCC=0.75 vs. 最佳基线0.08），包括基于最具信息量token的词汇阈值规则，证实存在真实的判别信号。信息增益分析揭示两类互补信号：flakiness标记主要出现在不稳定测试中，包括并发原语（async, await）、基于expectation的同步（expectation, fulfill）、错误传播（throws）和显式时间依赖（timeout, wait, now）；稳定性标记主要是纯同步测试的断言词汇（xctassertequal），作为反对flakiness的证据。错误分析表明，当flakiness隐藏在测试体之外的共享基础设施中，或异步构造用于确定性上下文时，模型会失败，这暴露了词汇预测的内在局限。

Conclusion: 该研究将基于词汇的flakiness检测扩展到Swift生态系统，刻画了其有效性（随机森林的高判别力与两类互补词汇信号）及其边界（共享基础设施与确定性上下文中的异步构造导致的失败），表明词汇方法虽有效但存在固有局限。

Abstract: Flaky tests produce non-deterministic outcomes without code change, eroding CI confidence and delaying deliveries. While vocabulary-based machine learning prediction has proven effective for Java and JavaScript, no study has evaluated it for Swift, a language whose testing style is dominated by UI and asynchronous code. We collect 91 flaky and 22,349 stable tests from 15 open-source Swift projects via re-execution and commit-history mining, then train five classifiers (Random Forest, Decision Tree, Naive Bayes, SVM, KNN) on TF-IDF unigram+bigram features under stratified 5-fold cross-validation. Random Forest achieves the best performance (Precision = 0.92, F1 = 0.86, AUC = 0.95) and substantially outperforms trivial baselines, among them a vocabulary-threshold rule applied to the most informative tokens, confirming a genuine discriminative signal (MCC = 0.75 vs. 0.08 for the best baseline). Information-gain analysis reveals two complementary signal types. Flakiness markers appear predominantly in unstable tests and comprise concurrency primitives (async, await), expectation-based synchronisation (expectation, fulfill), error propagation (throws), and explicit timing dependence (timeout, wait, now). Stability markers, chiefly the assertion vocabulary of plainly synchronous tests (xctassertequal), count as evidence against flakiness. Error analysis shows that the model fails when flakiness is hidden in shared infrastructure outside the test body or when async constructs are used in a deterministic context, exposing the intrinsic limit of lexical prediction. These results extend vocabulary-based flakiness detection to the Swift ecosystem and characterise both its effectiveness and its boundaries.

</details>


### [224] [Dynamic Conformance Testing of WebGPU Through Specification-Driven Mutation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25520)
*Mahya Samdaliri,Zhihao Yao,Kasthuri Jayarajah*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出LANTERN，一个规范引导的动态一致性测试框架，通过对WebGPU CTS测试进行语义感知变异来发现浏览器实现缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WebGPU是复杂的图形与计算API，官方CTS仅覆盖规范下的合规用法，通用模糊测试器又因复杂图形栈和多进程架构难以适用，故需要一个能针对语义边界与对抗性输入进行压力测试的方法。

Method: LANTERN从WebIDL定义中提取显式语法API规则，并从自然语言规范文本中恢复语义约束（如命令顺序、对象生命周期）；用选定规则引导基于AST定位的文本变换，生成有效与故意无效的CTS变体，并在AddressSanitizer插桩的Chromium上大规模执行测试。

Result: 在研究所处时期的Chromium版本中发现三个可复现缺陷，其中包括一处堆内存破坏（heap corruption）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，语法播种、语义感知的一致性测试变异是WebGPU测试中挖掘浏览器缺陷的有效途径。

Abstract: WebGPU is a low-level graphics and compute API that exposes modern GPU functionality to web applications. While the official WebGPU Conformance Test Suite (CTS) focuses on well-formed usage under the WebGPU specification, it is not designed to stress implementations with semantic edge cases or adversarial inputs. General-purpose fuzzers, in contrast, struggle with WebGPU because of its complex graphics stack and multi-process architecture. We introduce LANTERN, a specification-guided dynamic conformance testing framework that mutates CTS tests using constraints extracted from the WebGPU specification. LANTERN extracts explicit syntactic API rules from WebIDL definitions and recovers semantic constraints, such as command ordering and object lifetimes, from natural-language specification text. Selected rules guide AST-located textual transformations that generate both valid and intentionally invalid CTS variants. We execute the resulting tests at scale on AddressSanitizer-instrumented Chromium to discover bugs. Our evaluation discovers three reproducible bugs, including a heap corruption, in Chromium versions current at the time of study. These results demonstrate that syntactically seeded, semantics-aware mutation of conformance tests provides a way to uncover browser bugs during WebGPU testing.

</details>


### [225] [Evaluating Shaker for Flaky Test Detection in Python Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25528)
*Gabriela Leal,Denini Silva,Leopoldo Teixeira*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次在Python上实证评估Shaker工具，发现在预算匹配配对设计下，Shaker相对简单重执行在非顺序依赖型flaky测试检测上无统计显著优势（37.2% vs. 35.8%），原因在于多数基准测试在独立硬件上无法复现，且真正复现的测试主要由网络交互与随机性而非并发导致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flaky测试在代码未变时通过或失败不确定，侵蚀测试套件可信度并推高失败成本。Shaker通过注入资源竞争（CPU、内存、I/O压力）放大并发引起的非确定性，在Java和Android基准上被报告可检测95%的flaky测试，远高于普通重执行的37.5%。但该工具尚未在Python上进行实证验证，本文旨在填补这一空白，检验Shaker跨语言的泛化性与有效性。

Method: 从Gruber等人的ground-truth数据集抽取非顺序依赖型flaky测试，采用预算匹配的配对设计，将Shaker与ReRun基线对比：每个测试共137个，各在两种技术下运行100次，执行次数相同。通过McNemar精确检验判断检测率差异是否显著，并分析测试可复现性及flaky诱因分布。

Result: 按Java和Android配置的Shaker相对普通重执行无统计显著检测优势（37.2% vs. 35.8%；McNemar精确p = 0.84）。两项发现解释原因：一是不到一半的ground-truth flaky测试在独立硬件上能复现为flaky，多数无法复现的测试在100次运行中一次都不发散；二是能复现的测试主要由网络交互和随机性主导，而非Shaker针对的并发因素。

Conclusion: Shaker在Python上未展现跨语言有效性，其检测优势依赖于特定flaky成因和可复现环境。本文进一步揭示领域性风险：跨执行环境复用flaky测试ground-truth会悄然将真实flaky测试转化为表观真阴性，从而降低任何工具测得的召回率，因此评估需警惕基准环境的适用性与可移植性。

Abstract: Flaky tests pass or fail non-deterministically on unchanged code, eroding trust in test suites and inflating the cost of every failure. Shaker detects them by injecting resource contention (CPU, memory, and I/O stress) to amplify non-determinism caused by concurrent execution, and was reported to detect 95% of the flaky tests in a Java and Android benchmark against 37.5% for plain re-execution (ReRun). We present the first empirical evaluation of Shaker for Python. Drawing non-order-dependent flaky tests from the ground-truth dataset of Gruber et al., we compare Shaker against a budget-matched ReRun baseline in a paired design, giving both techniques the same number of test executions: Each of 137 tests is run 100 times under each. As configured for Java and Android, Shaker provides no statistically significant detection advantage over plain re-execution (37.2% vs. 35.8%; McNemar exact p = 0.84). Two findings explain why. First, fewer than half of the ground-truth flaky tests reproduce as flaky at all on independent hardware under either technique, and most of the tests that fail to reproduce never diverge once across 100 runs. Second, the tests that do reproduce are dominated by flakiness from network interactions and randomness rather than the concurrency Shaker targets. Beyond the tool, this exposes a broader hazard for the field: reusing a flaky-test ground truth across execution environments silently converts genuine flaky tests into apparent true negatives, deflating any tool's measured recall.

</details>


### [226] [An Empirical Analysis of Cross-OS Portability Issues in Python Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25531)
*Denini Silva,MohamadAli Farahat,Marcelo d'Amorim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 首个针对Python跨操作系统可移植性问题的大规模实证研究，通过跨OS测试复执行与GitHub issue人工分析，建立失败分类体系并评估静态工具与大模型检测修复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python虽为跨平台语言，但实际应用在不同操作系统部署时常出现可移植性失败，而学界缺乏对这类问题的系统性理解与基线数据。

Method: 分析2,042个开源仓库，采用两种互补方法：(1)对500个项目进行系统性跨OS测试复执行；(2)人工分析240个GitHub issue，并构建包含7大主类、24个子类、15种诊断特征与4种修复模式的分类体系，同时评估静态分析工具与LLM的表现。

Result: 跨平台测试显示11.2%项目存在操作系统相关测试失败；确认102个真实可移植性问题（涉及另外95个项目）；现有静态分析工具支持极少，LLM在结构化指导下识别准确率40-79%、修复成功率50-77%；提交33个PR，17个被合并、0个被拒。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为理解和解决Python跨OS可移植性问题建立全面基线，为开发者、工具设计者及研究社区提供可操作的见解。

Abstract: While Python is designed as a cross-platform language, real-world applications encounter portability failures when deployed across different operating systems. We present the first large-scale empirical study of cross-OS portability issues in Python, analyzing 2,042 open-source repositories using two complementary approaches: systematic cross-OS test reexecution and manual analysis of GitHub issues. Our cross-platform testing of 500 projects reveals that 11.2% exhibit OS-dependent test failures. Through systematic analysis of 240 GitHub issues, we confirm 102 genuine portability problems spanning 95 additional projects. We develop a comprehensive taxonomy identifying 7 primary failure categories - with file/directory operations, process management, and library dependencies being most prevalent - along with 24 distinct sub-categories, 15 diagnostic signatures, and 4 systematic repair patterns. Our evaluation reveals that existing static analysis tools provide minimal support for portability detection, while large language models achieve 40-79% accuracy in identifying issues and 50-77% success in generating fixes when provided with structured guidance. Through 33 contributed pull requests, we demonstrate practical applicability and developer acceptance (17 merged, zero rejected) of our findings. This work establishes the first comprehensive baseline for understanding and addressing cross-OS portability issues in Python, providing actionable insights for developers, tool designers, and the broader research community.

</details>


### [227] [Understanding Maintenance and Support in a Community-Driven Scientific Workflow Ecosystem: A Cross-Space Study of Galaxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25587)
*Khairul Alam,Kowsik Roy,Md Shamimur Rahman,Banani Roy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对Galaxy科学工作流系统开展大规模实证研究，基于11,762条GitHub issue、52,203个pull request和6,235条社区论坛讨论，刻画其维护与支持议题，分析解决结果与解决时间的影响因素，并考察跨空间维护制品间的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Galaxy作为社区驱动的科学工作流系统，其可持续性依赖软件、工具、工作流、基础设施、文档与用户支持等多个层面的持续维护。然而维护知识分散在开发空间与社区支持空间中，导致难以理解维护对象、维护制品如何被解决，以及用户关切与仓库级开发之间的联系。

Method: 采用BERTopic主题建模，对三类维护制品进行主题识别；开展解决分析，考察协调、诊断、贡献者、自动化与参与等特征与制品是否被解决及解决速度的关联；并对GitHub与社区论坛之间的显式关系及潜在（语义与技术）关联进行追溯分析。

Result: 识别出9个issue主题、14个pull request主题和14个论坛主题，覆盖工作流执行、数据管理、工具与依赖、基础设施、测试、科学资源、文档与用户支持等维护版图。解决分析显示上述各类特征与解决结果和解决时间存在不同关联。显式可追溯性有限：16,426个已解决的显式关系中97.77%发生在GitHub内部，仅294个连接GitHub制品与社区论坛讨论，尽管跨空间存在额外的语义与技术相关性。

Conclusion: Galaxy的维护是一种分布式生态系统级过程。研究指出在诊断性报告、生命周期感知的分诊、跨空间可追溯性以及社区支持知识复用方面的改进机会。

Abstract: Galaxy is a widely used, community-driven scientific workflow system whose sustainability depends on continuous maintenance across its software, tools, workflows, infrastructure, documentation, and user-support ecosystem. However, maintenance knowledge in Galaxy is distributed across development and community-support spaces, making it difficult to understand what is maintained, how maintenance artifacts are resolved, and how user-facing concerns connect to repository-level development. We conduct a large-scale empirical study of Galaxy using 11,762 GitHub issues, 52,203 pull requests, and 6,235 Community Forum discussions. We characterize maintenance and support concerns, examine factors associated with resolution outcomes and resolution time, and investigate explicit and candidate connections among maintenance artifacts across these spaces.
  Using BERTopic modeling, we identify nine issue topics, 14 pull-request topics, and 14 forum topics, revealing a maintenance landscape spanning workflow execution, data management, tools and dependencies, infrastructure, testing, scientific resources, documentation, and user support. Resolution analyses show that coordination, diagnostic, contributor, automation, and engagement characteristics exhibit different associations with whether artifacts are resolved and how quickly resolution occurs. We further find limited explicit traceability between development and support spaces: 97.77\% of 16,426 resolved explicit relationships occur within GitHub, while only 294 connect GitHub artifacts with Community Forum discussions, despite additional semantic and technical relatedness across these spaces. Together, these findings characterize Galaxy maintenance as a distributed ecosystem-level process and identify opportunities to improve diagnostic reporting, lifecycle-aware triage, cross-space traceability, and the reuse of community-support knowledge.

</details>


### [228] [What Was Once Learned May Need to Be Unlearned: Machine Unlearning for Deprecated API Knowledge in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25786)
*Jin Liu,Yanzhong He,Guancheng Lin,Xiao Liu,Jacky Wai Keung,Xiao Yu,Xiaoxue Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究面向废弃API知识的机器遗忘方法，构建行为驱动的基准MUDAPIBench（145组废弃-现行API映射、8个Python库、7000+模型特定实例），在3个代码LLM上评测8种遗忘方法，发现Gradient Difference在遗忘效果与能力保持间取得最佳折中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码补全LLM因预训练语料含历史版本代码而可能生成废弃API。现有方法（推理时干预、模型编辑、机器遗忘）在多种合理补全并存时，预定义替换过于受限；且既有研究很少验证模型是否真的表现出目标废弃行为，也少评估对其他API的意外影响。

Method: 开展面向废弃API知识的机器遗忘系统性实证研究，构建MUDAPIBench行为驱动基准：跨8个Python库、145组废弃到最新API映射、7000+模型特定实例，仅保留原始模型确实生成目标废弃API的实例；在3个代码LLM上评测8种代表性遗忘方法，覆盖废弃API遗忘、最新API生成、其他及无关API行为保持、通用代码生成能力和效率五个维度。

Result: Gradient Difference（GD）综合权衡最佳：在中等计算开销下抑制废弃API并保持其他能力；不同库之间差异显著；模型训练数据截止后才被废弃的API更难遗忘；逐层分析显示GD以相对受控的内部变化实现有效遗忘。

Conclusion: 机器遗忘用于废弃API知识具有可行性，GD是较优方案；同时强调需以行为驱动方式构建基准、全面评估遗忘的副作用，并关注库间差异与训练截止时间对遗忘难度的影响。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) for code completion may generate deprecated APIs because their pre-training corpora contain code from historical library versions. Existing approaches use inference-time intervention, model editing, or machine unlearning, but multiple plausible completions make predefined replacements restrictive. Moreover, existing studies rarely verify whether models exhibit the targeted deprecated behavior or evaluate unintended changes to other APIs.
  We conduct a systematic empirical study of machine unlearning for deprecated API knowledge and construct MUDAPIBench, a behavior-grounded benchmark with over 7,000 model-specific instances derived from 145 deprecated-to-up-to-date API mappings across eight Python libraries. Instances are retained only when the original model generates the target deprecated API. We evaluate eight representative unlearning methods across three code LLMs on deprecated API forgetting, up-to-date API generation, preservation of other and unrelated API behaviors, general code-generation capability, and efficiency.
  Results show that Gradient Difference (GD) provides the best overall trade-off, suppressing deprecated APIs while preserving other capabilities with moderate computational costs. Further analyses reveal substantial variation across libraries and show that APIs deprecated after the model's training-data cutoff are harder to forget. Layer-wise analyses indicate that GD achieves effective forgetting with comparatively controlled internal changes.

</details>


### [229] [Confidence-Guided Cross-Modal Knowledge Transfer for Multimodal Anomaly Detection in Microservice Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25856)
*Peipeng Wang,Xiuguo Zhang,Lihua Zhang,Zhiying Cao,Jingchun Zhou,Zheng Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CMT-AD方法，通过置信度引导的跨模态知识迁移，在统一深度聚类框架下联合建模指标与日志以实现多模态异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态异常检测中有效利用可靠的跨模态信息面临两大挑战：一是各模态受负载波动等因素影响，可靠性动态变化；二是多模态数据在结构与语义上存在异质性。

Method: 在统一深度聚类框架中联合建模指标和日志，通过软聚类分布估计模态可靠性，将聚类不确定性量化为置信度分数；以置信度分数引导模型分析各模态在跨模态交互中的贡献，并用高置信度模态补充低置信度模态；引入门控中间模态及结构一致性与语义一致性约束，对齐原始模态与中间模态以保留跨模态相似结构和语义分布；同时加入模态内与跨模态正则项以增强聚类紧致性并缓解负迁移。

Result: 在三个大规模数据集上进行评估，CMT-AD优于现有最先进方法，F1分数高于0.9。

Conclusion: 置信度引导的跨模态知识迁移结合统一深度聚类与一致性约束，能有效应对模态可靠性动态变化和跨模态异质性，提升多模态异常检测性能。

Abstract: Accurate anomaly detection is essential for reliable and secure operations of microservice systems. While an increasing number of studies have shifted from unimodal modeling to multimodal interaction and fusion, effectively leveraging reliable cross-modal information remains challenging. The challenge primarily stems from two aspects. Firstly, different modalities are influenced by factors like load fluctuations, leading to dynamically changing reliability. Secondly, multimodal data exhibit heterogeneity in both structure and semantics. Therefore, we propose a confidence-guided Cross-Modal knowledge Transfer method for multimodal Anomaly Detection (CMT-AD). It jointly models metrics and logs within a unified deep clustering framework and estimates modality reliability through the soft clustering distributions, where clustering uncertainty is quantified into confidence scores. Guided by these confidence scores, the model actively analyzes the contributions of each modality in cross-modal interactions and supplements low-confidence modalities with knowledge from high-confidence ones. To further mitigate cross-modal heterogeneity, we introduce a gated intermediate modality and design structural and semantic consistency constraints that align the original modalities with the intermediate modality to preserve similarity structures and semantic distributions across modalities. Furthermore, intra-modal and cross-modal regularization terms are incorporated to enhance cluster compactness and mitigate negative transfer. We evaluated CMT-AD on three large-scale datasets, and the results demonstrate that CMT-AD outperforms state-of-the-art approaches and achieves an F1-score higher than 0.9.

</details>


### [230] [When Should Dependency Updates Invoke Repair Agents? A Lightweight Routing Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25911)
*Liheng Fan,Jialun Yin,Yuzhi Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 DepFixRouter，一个在使用昂贵仓库级编码代理之前对依赖更新 PR 进行路由的轻量分类器，利用创建时文本与元数据信号预测兼容性修复可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 依赖更新 PR 频繁且大多为常规操作，仅少数需要非平凡的兼容性修复。对所有更新都调用仓库级编码代理会浪费模型调用、CI 时间、仓库上下文与审查注意力，因此需要在代理介入前进行升级路由。

Method: 将问题表述为 pre-agent 路由问题；基于 497 个已标注 GitHub 依赖更新候选，使用仅含 PR 标题与 bot/依赖标志的创建时安全 LinearSVC 进行排序，并与使用完整历史信号的回溯性特征作对比。

Result: 497 个候选中有 72 个需要实质性修复；创建时 LinearSVC 达到修复 F1 0.488，前 20% 路由召回 51.4%，将每次捕获修复的调用从 6.90 降至 2.68；加入回溯全历史信号后前 20% 召回升至 65.3%，表明 PR 历史中存在显著事后泄漏；60 例诊断代理试点中，路由门控将 LLM 调用减少 66.7%、token 减少 66.1%。

Conclusion: DepFixRouter 可作为常规依赖更新自动化与昂贵仓库级代理之间的轻量升级层，实现预算感知的维护，且部署时路由不依赖回溯性修复证据。

Abstract: Dependency-update pull requests are frequent and mostly routine, but a small subset requires non-trivial compatibility repair. Recent repository-level coding agents make such repair increasingly plausible, yet invoking them on every dependency update wastes model calls, CI time, repository context, and review attention. We frame this as a pre-agent routing problem: deciding which dependency-update pull requests should be escalated before downstream diagnosis or repair attempts. We introduce DepFixRouter, a lightweight router that ranks dependency updates by historical compatibility-repair likelihood using creation-time textual and metadata signals. On 497 labeled GitHub dependency-update candidates, only 72 require substantive repair. A creation-time-safe LinearSVC using only PR titles and bot/dependency flags reaches 0.488 repair F1 and captures 51.4% of repairs within the top 20% routed pull requests, improving calls per captured repair from 6.90 under route-all or random policies to 2.68. Retrospective full-history signals improve top-20% recall to 65.3%, revealing substantial hindsight leakage in pull-request histories rather than deployment-time routing utility. In a 60-case diagnosis-agent pilot, router-gated diagnosis reduces actual LLM calls by 66.7% and tokens by 66.1%, suggesting budgetaware escalation while measuring diagnosis rather than patch generation. DepFixRouter can serve as a lightweight escalation layer between routine dependency-update automation and expensive repository-level agents, enabling budget-aware maintenance without relying on retrospective repair evidence for deployment-time routing.

</details>


### [231] [Compiling Sufficient Governance Context from Declared Losses and Reachable States: Exact Observation-Contract Synthesis with Cardinality and Cost Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26016)
*Gaston Besanson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出并验证"最小充分治理上下文"，用充分性检查、极小/最小基数或最小成本契约综合（穷举或SAT/MaxSAT编码），在构造域上发现不可省核心不充分、存在两个不同reduct、成本模型可/不可区分替代方案等结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 给定有限可达状态模型、确定性判定与候选可观测属性时，需要明确哪些观测契约足以确定判定，哪些属性单独不可省，以及如何按基数或成本目标在充分契约中选择；同时需澄清穷举与SAT/MaxSAT综合的可行性与范围边界。

Method: 定义观测契约（属性集，其值在所有可达状态上决定判定）与权威契约（在目标与界下选择并绑定到gate schema）。穷举可行时综合所有包含极小充分契约；否则用SAT/MaxSAT编码求最小基数或最小成本契约，并直接检查充分性。AuthorityBench在三个域比较四个基线；失败给反例，成功给检查摘要而非可移植证书。

Result: 在构造的code/cloud域中，单独不可省核心作为观测契约不充分，存在两个不同reduct，预注册成本模型可精确区分；在第二个更大构造域中同样模式重现，但成本模型不能区分替代方案，报告为完全解释的成本并列。穷举在注册超时内确认不可行，SAT/MaxSAT在远低于一秒内求解；MaxSAT相较普通SAT未测得基数优势。AuthorityBench的declared-only基线在所有域均非精确充分。

Conclusion: 该工作将充分治理上下文的综合与认证形式化，并通过充分性检查、极小/最小契约选择与基准比较给出编译器聚焦范围内的证据；端到端独立案例研究列为后续注册工作，本文不作声明。

Abstract: We call the object this paper derives and certifies a minimal sufficient governance context: given a finite reachable-state model, a deterministic declared verdict, and candidate observable attributes, we compute sufficient observation sets, distinguish attributes that are individually indispensable from contracts that are jointly sufficient, and select among sufficient contracts under a cardinality or declared-cost objective. An observation contract is a set of candidate attributes whose values determine the declared verdict on every reachable state; an authority contract is one selected under an objective and bound to a gate schema. We synthesize every inclusion-minimal sufficient contract where exhaustive enumeration is affordable, and a minimum-cardinality or minimum-cost contract by SAT/MaxSAT encoding otherwise, checking sufficiency directly. On a constructed code/cloud domain, the individually-indispensable core is not sufficient as an observation contract and two distinct reducts exist; a preregistered cost model separates them exactly. On a second, larger, constructed domain, the same pattern recurs, but that domain's cost model does not separate the alternatives: a fully explained cost tie, reported as found. We measure discernibility-family scaling where exhaustive enumeration is confirmed infeasible within a registered timeout, while SAT/MaxSAT synthesis solves in well under a second; MaxSAT showed no measured cardinality advantage over plain SAT. AuthorityBench compares four baselines across three domains; the declared-only baseline is not exactly sufficient on any. Every selected contract is checked for sufficiency, with a counterexample on failure and a check summary, not a portable certificate, on success -- the compiler-focused scope of a two-scope table; an independently specified end-to-end case study is registered follow-up work, not claimed here.

</details>


### [232] [On Behavioral Alignment of Model-Code and Human-Code Understandability via Behavioral Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26101)
*Xiaokai Rong,Aashish Yadavally,Anh H. N. Nguyen,Hridya Dhulipala,Tien N. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出代码可理解性是读者与代码交互的关系属性，将"读者"从人类扩展到LLM，并通过四种行为代理指标(BPMU)研究人类与模型的代码可理解性行为对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究多从以人为中心或以代码为中心的单一视角研究代码可理解性，忽视了其作为读者与代码交互产生的关系属性；同时LLM在软件工程中日益普及，因此需要将"读者"概念泛化，区分人类与模型的代码可理解性并检验二者行为是否对齐。

Method: 基于前期研究中包含多类参与者群体人类可理解性评分的数据集，评估多个开源与闭源LLM；提出模型-代码可理解性的四种行为代理BPMU：P0基于程序理解问答，P1–P3基于程序意图摘要（源自提出的语义自一致性）；并进行分层分析与角色条件提示实验。

Result: LLM与一般人类代码可理解性的行为对齐优于以往浅层机器学习基线；模型可理解性与专业开发者对齐最强，与其他学生群体显著较弱；角色条件提示未能改善对后者的对齐；语义自一致性是可靠且可扩展的行为代理指标。

Conclusion: 应以关系视角看待代码可理解性并区分人类与模型可理解性；当前LLM难以准确模拟不同专业水平人类读者的视角，而语义自一致性可作为量化模型代码可理解性的通用代理，对软件工程研究与实践具有广泛意义。

Abstract: Code understandability is a critical aspect of software quality. Prior research has largely focused on this attribute from a human-centric or code-centric perspective, while it should be viewed as a relational property arising from the interaction between a reader and the code. With the increasing adoption of large language models in software engineering, we posit that the notion of "reader" should be generalized to encompass both humans and models. Building on this relational perspective, we extend the concept of code understandability to distinguish between human and model code understandability, aiming to investigate the behavioral alignment between the two. To this end, we use a dataset from a prior study containing human-rated judgments of understandability across diverse participant groups, and evaluate multiple open and closed-source LLMs. To operationalize such behavioral alignment, we introduce four behavioral proxies of model-code understandability (BPMU): P0, based on program comprehension-focused question answering; and P1--P3, based on program intent summarization (derived from our proposed semantic self-consistency). Our findings show that LLMs exhibit stronger behavioral alignment with general human code understandability than previously used shallow machine learning baselines. Stratified analyses further reveal that model code understandability aligns most closely with professional developers, but significantly less with other student groups. Role-conditioned prompting does not improve the alignment for the latter, suggesting that current LLMs cannot accurately mimic the perspectives of human readers with varying expertise. Finally, we demonstrate that semantic self-consistency is a reliable and extensible measure to be used as a behavioral proxy for quantifying model code understandability, with broad implications in both software engineering research and practice.

</details>


### [233] [Post-Hoc Attention Steering of Large Language Models for Robust Code Understanding under Obfuscation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26102)
*Xiaokai Rong,Aashish Yadavally,Tien N. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CodeSteer通过推理时注意力引导，将LLM注意力重分配到语义相关程序元素，显著提升混淆代码理解性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码混淆广泛用于软件和恶意软件以掩盖程序逻辑，给人工分析和自动化工具带来挑战。LLM虽在代码理解上表现强，但对混淆的鲁棒性研究不足；初步研究显示LLM在混淆代码上性能明显下降，说明其依赖表层词法线索而非深层语义推理。

Method: 提出CodeSteer，一种注意力引导方法，将模型注意力重新分配到语义相关的程序元素：用于输出预测的后向切片和用于执行推理的控制流路径。方法结合轻量级程序分析与推理时注意力引导，引导LLM关注程序核心的输入到输出依赖关系。

Result: 在多种模型和数据集上的实验表明，CodeSteer显著提升混淆代码上的性能，常能恢复到与未混淆程序相当的水平；缓冲区溢出检测案例研究展示了其实际效用。

Conclusion: CodeSteer证明注意力引导可缓解LLM对混淆代码的脆弱性，对恶意软件/漏洞分析与混淆代码逆向工程具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Code obfuscation is widely used in software systems and malware to conceal program logic and hinder analysis, posing significant challenges for both human developers and automated tools. While large language models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in code understanding, their robustness to obfuscation remains poorly understood. Our preliminary study shows that LLM performance significantly degrades on obfuscated code, suggesting a reliance on superficial lexical cues rather than deep semantic reasoning. To address this limitation, we propose CodeSteer, a novel attention steering approach that reallocates model attention toward semantically relevant program elements, including backward slices for output prediction and control-flow paths for execution reasoning. Our method integrates lightweight program analysis with inference-time attention steering to guide LLMs toward the core input-to-output dependencies of a program. Experiments across multiple models and datasets demonstrate that CodeSteer significantly improves performance on obfuscated code, often recovering comparable accuracy to the level of unobfuscated programs. We also show CodeSteer's practical utility through a case study on buffer overflow detection, highlighting its potential for malware/vulnerability analysis and reverse engineering of obfuscated code.

</details>


### [234] [Why Do LLMs Fail at OCL Generation? A Graph Reasoning Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26122)
*Hamza Attarwala,Moataz Chouchen,Mohammad Hamdaqa,Omar Alam*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究将LLM生成OCL约束的任务框定为UML类图上的图推理问题，通过PathOCL数据集对六个SOTA模型进行实证评估，发现导航深度与结构复杂度是性能下降的主因，词汇相似度影响有限，图感知提示仅带来部分改进，表明结构推理而非语言因素是当前LLM在模型驱动工程任务中的核心瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于LLM从自然语言规约与UML类图生成OCL约束的研究多聚焦于提升准确率，缺乏对模型失败原因的理解。本文旨在探究LLM在OCL生成中失败的深层原因。

Method: 将OCL生成任务重新框定为UML类图上的图推理问题，基于PathOCL数据集对六种先进LLM进行实证评估，分析UML结构属性（如导航深度、模型复杂度）、词汇相似度、提示排序策略以及图感知提示对OCL正确性的影响。

Result: OCL生成性能随导航深度和结构复杂度增加而显著下降；词汇相似度影响有限；UML元素的文本排序会影响性能；基于图的提示带来部分提升，但无法消除结构推理错误。

Conclusion: OCL生成主要受图推理能力限制而非纯语言因素制约，结构推理是当前LLM在模型驱动工程任务中的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate Object Constraint Language (OCL) constraints from natural language specifications and UML class diagrams. However, existing work mainly focuses on improving accuracy, with limited understanding of why these models fail. Aims. This study investigates the underlying causes of LLM failures in OCL generation, framing the task as a graph reasoning problem over UML class diagrams. Method. We conduct an empirical evaluation using the PathOCL dataset across six state-of-the-art LLMs. We analyze the impact of UML structural properties (e.g., navigation depth and model complexity), lexical similarity, prompt ordering strategies, and graph-aware prompting on OCL correctness. Results. We find that OCL generation performance significantly degrades with increasing navigation depth and structural complexity. Lexical similarity has limited influence, while textual ordering of UML elements affects performance. Graph-based prompting yields partial improvements but does not eliminate structural reasoning errors. Conclusions. OCL generation is primarily constrained by graph reasoning limitations rather than purely linguistic factors. These results highlight structural reasoning as a key bottleneck for current LLMs in model-driven engineering tasks.

</details>


### [235] [A Large-Scale Longitudinal Study of Multi-CI Service Adoption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26181)
*Taher A. Ghaleb,Rasha Bin-Thalab*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文对13.5万余个GitHub仓库、7种语言、8种CI服务开展大规模纵向研究，刻画多CI服务的采用、演进与弃用生命周期，揭示CI服务选择是主导因素，向GitHub Actions的迁移是平台级同步转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CI采用已从单一实践演变为多服务竞争格局，许多项目不再依赖单一CI服务，但多服务行为在项目生命周期中如何演变尚不清楚。既有研究多基于单一服务、特定语言或开发者访谈考察CI采用与迁移，多服务使用的生命周期仍未被充分探索。

Method: 对135227个GitHub仓库（覆盖7种编程语言、8种CI服务）进行大规模纵向研究，结合定量与定性分析，刻画CI服务的采用、演进与弃用；并分析CI配置提交的理由说明。

Result: 约五分之一仓库使用多个CI服务，且多属迁移过渡而非长期并行使用；CI服务选择在采用、复杂度、维护与投入度上均为主导因素，语言效应仅限特定情形；向GitHub Actions的转变是平台级同步迁移，2020年在所有研究语言中首次采用率均超过50%；Travis CI在定价变更后衰退成为最强弃用信号；仅约11%的提交陈述战略性理由，表明提交信息更多反映运维维护模式而非战略意图；多CI采用主要与项目成熟度相关，而弃用更与服务身份而非仓库特征相关。

Conclusion: 多CI服务采用是项目成熟度的体现，弃用则更受服务身份驱动；研究为CI工具设计、迁移支持及演进型软件交付基础设施的未来研究提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Continuous Integration (CI) adoption has evolved from a single practice into a broad landscape of competing services, and many projects no longer rely on a single CI service. However, how this multi-service behavior evolves over a project's lifetime remains unknown. Prior work has examined CI adoption and migration through individual services, specific languages, or developer interviews, leaving the lifecycle of multi-service usage underexplored. In this paper, we conduct a large-scale longitudinal study of multi-CI service adoption covering 135227 GitHub repositories from seven programming languages and involving eight CI services. We characterize how CI services are adopted, evolved, and abandoned over time through quantitative and qualitative analyses. About one in five repositories use multiple CI services, often transitional during migration rather than for sustained parallel use. CI service choice is the dominant factor across adoption, complexity, maintenance, and engagement, with language effects limited to specific cases. Our results reveal that the shift to GitHub Actions was a synchronized platform-level transition, crossing 50\% of first adoptions in 2020 across all studied languages, while the decline of Travis CI following its pricing change became the strongest abandonment signal. Our analysis of CI configuration rationales shows that activity is mostly reactive: only about 11\% of commits state a strategic reason, indicating that commit messages capture operational maintenance patterns more reliably than strategic intent. Finally, we find that multi-CI adoption is primarily associated with project maturity, whereas abandonment is more strongly associated with service identity than repository characteristics. These findings provide empirical evidence for CI tooling, migration support, and future research on managing evolving software delivery infrastructure.

</details>


### [236] [WatchPoint: Executable User Feedback for Real-World Agentic Web Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26204)
*Guanqun Yang,Wei Yang,Xueqing Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 WatchPoint——一个模拟真实开发者调试行为的"模拟用户"系统，通过生成并执行诊断脚本对运行中的应用进行结构化观测，指导代码模型重试，在 Web-Bench 上恢复 57.6% 的任务，与人类测试者的 54.5% 相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体的反馈机制主要依赖截图、LLM 评分或自然语言纠正，很少像真实开发者那样与运行中的应用交互（点击按钮、检查计算样式、运行诊断命令）。这种差距使得智能体难以理解代码失败的真正原因，尤其缺乏对多文件、序列依赖的 Web 项目的端到端诊断能力。

Method: 提出 WatchPoint 模拟用户系统：模仿真实开发者行为，针对运行中的应用自动生成并执行诊断脚本，产出结构化观测结果，用于引导编码模型的重试。评测环境为 Web-Bench，包含 50 个多文件 Web 项目、1,000 个顺序依赖任务，并由确定性的端到端测试验证。

Result: WatchPoint 可恢复其所诊断任务的 57.6%；对照用户研究显示人类测试者恢复率为 54.5%，两者相当，验证了模拟的真实性。此外识别出一类能力差距模式，决定模拟用户反馈在何时有益、何时应被抑制。

Conclusion: 自动化诊断脚本可在顺序式 Web 开发任务中替代交互式人工测试，模拟用户反馈能有效提升编码智能体的任务恢复能力，但其效果受特定能力差距模式调节，应在合适场景下选择性使用。

Abstract: When a professional web developer's code fails a test, they do not simply re-read the stack trace. They open the application in a browser, click buttons, inspect computed styles, and run diagnostic commands to understand what went wrong. Existing feedback mechanisms for coding agents rely on screenshots, LLM-as-a-judge scoring, or natural-language corrections, but few interact with the live application the way a developer would. We introduce WatchPoint, a simulated-user system that mimics real developer behavior by generating and executing diagnostic scripts against the running application, producing structured observations that guide the coding model's retry. Unlike prior approaches that target single-file edits or evaluate using non-executable metrics, we operate on Web-Bench, a benchmark of 50 multi-file web projects comprising 1,000 sequentially dependent tasks, verified by deterministic end-to-end tests. WatchPoint recovers 57.6% of the tasks it diagnoses, and a controlled user study confirms the simulation's realism: human testers achieve a comparable recovery rate (54.5%), providing evidence that automated diagnostic scripts can substitute for interactive human testing on sequential web development tasks. We further identify a pattern of capability gaps that governs when simulated-user feedback is helpful and when it should be withheld.

</details>


### [237] [Reducing Hallucinations in Large Language Models Through Integrated Self-Verification and Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26229)
*Ashly Joseph*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CoVe-RAG+框架，将链式验证（CoVe）与检索增强生成（RAG）相结合，用于抑制LLM幻觉。该框架在工程标准、CAD信息、仿真报告等外部权威来源中开展迭代自验证。在CAD文档、标准合规性验证与历史设计数据复用等任务上，事实准确率较基线CoVe和RAG提升28%，并通过可解释的验证报告和来源可追溯性提升用户信任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已逐步用于CAD文档、标准合规性校验、知识检索等高级工程任务，但易产生幻觉——输出看似可信却无上下文依据，这严重限制其在高精度、高合规要求的工程场景中的可信度。因此需要一种机制来抑制幻觉。

Method: 提出CoVe-RAG+统一框架：将Chain-of-Verification（CoVe）与Retrieval-Augmented Generation（RAG）集成；支持在外部权威来源（工程标准、CAD信息、仿真报告）中验证LLM输出；并采用迭代式自验证流程对关键论断进行核验。

Result: 在CAD模型文档、标准合规性验证、历史设计数据复用等工程任务上评估：事实准确率相较基线CoVe与RAG方法提升28%；同时通过可解释的验证报告和来源可追溯性增强用户信心。

Conclusion: CoVe-RAG+为在事实准确性至关重要的工程设计流程中部署LLM提供了一种可扩展且可靠的方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are progressively used for advanced engineering tasks, includes Computer-Aided Design (CAD) documentation, standards compliance verification, and knowledge retrieval. Still, they are prone to produce hallucinations, outputs that seem convincing but aren't based on context that limit their trustworthiness in high-end engineering applications where precision and compliance are crucial. The paper introduces CoVe-RAG+, a unified framework that integrates Chain-of-Verification (CoVe) with Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to mitigate hallucinations in the results generated by large language models (LLMs). CoVe-RAG+ supports LLM verification in external sources of authority, such as engineering standards, CAD information, and simulation reports, while applying an iterative self-verification process to validate important claims. CoVe-RAG+ is assessed on engineering activities such as CAD model documentation, standards compliance verification, and the reutilization of historical design data. Experimental findings indicate a 28% improvement in factual accuracy relative to baseline CoVe and RAG methodologies. Moreover, CoVe-RAG+ strengthens user confidence by providing elucidative verification reports and source traceability. The findings indicate that CoVe-RAG+ provides a scalable and reliable option for implementing LLMs in engineering design processes where factual accuracy is critical.

</details>


### [238] [CANcept: Model-based CAN Traffic Generation and Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26263)
*Lino Wertz,Junes Sheikhi,Florian Fehrle,Adrian Rupp,Tianhai Liu,Philipp Kern,Carsten Sinz,Bernhard Beckert*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出开源基于模型的工具（\toolName{}），用流量调度模型（TSM）与基于DBC的通信模型（DCM）统一描述与执行CAN报文时序与内容变换，支持生成、重放与操纵CAN流量。初步评估表明其能实现设定场景，时序偏差低，并在高流量率下执行稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试基于CAN总线的软件中与时间相关的安全属性，需要对发送数据和通信时序进行精确控制；现有开源工具多以底层脚本编码此类场景，难以维护与演化。

Method: 基于模型的工具：流量调度模型（TSM）定义报文时序与内容变换，基于DBC的通信模型（DCM）定义报文级流量与CAN帧之间的编解码；两者在一个执行机制中结合，用于生成与操纵流量，并支持经变换轨迹的重放。

Result: 初步评估显示，\toolName{} 能够实现所指定的场景，时序偏差低，并且在流量速率升高时仍保持稳定执行。

Conclusion: \toolName{} 以模型驱动方式替代低层脚本，简化了CAN时序相关安全属性测试场景的规范、生成、重放与操纵，具备良好时序精度与稳定性。

Abstract: Testing timing-related safety properties of Controller Area Network (CAN)-based software requires precise control over transmitted data and communication timing. Existing open-source tools typically encode such scenarios in low-level scripts, making them difficult to maintain and evolve. We present \toolName{}, an open-source model-based tool for specifying, generating, replaying, and manipulating CAN traffic. A traffic schedule model (TSM) defines message timing and content transformations, and a DBC-based communication model (DCM) defines encoding and decoding between message-level traffic and CAN frames. \toolName{} combines both models in one execution mechanism for generated and manipulated traffic, including replay of transformed traces. A preliminary evaluation indicates that \toolName{} realizes the specified scenarios, with low timing deviation and stable execution under elevated traffic rates.

</details>


### [239] [On the Lexical Superstition of Large Language Models for Code Comprehension: Re-evaluation on Code of Low Lexical Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26388)
*Xin Shen,San-Zhuo Xi,Yali Du,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出语义保持的标识符重命名框架Face/Off，发现当前LLM在代码理解任务中普遍过度依赖标识符的词法线索，即使程序结构不变，性能也随标识符信息被移除或误导而下降，且该问题在提示与微调干预下依然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标识符名称在自然代码中具有统计信息量，但其信息并不总是可靠。作者关心的是：当程序结构保持不变、仅重命名标识符时，当前LLM是否会不成比例地依赖词法线索，从而偏离对代码语义的真实理解。

Method: 引入Face/Off——一个语义保持的标识符重命名框架，在多个模型和代码理解任务上设置递进的命名条件（如逐步移除或替换为误导性标识符），并施加代表性的提示工程与微调干预；同时设计类型推断对照实验，检验答案在无需目标名称即可局部恢复时的命名效应。

Result: 词法过度依赖在所评估的模型与主要任务中普遍存在：随着标识符信息被移除或变得误导，性能总体下降，输出常偏向误导性名称所暗示的含义；该模式在提示与微调干预下持续存在。类型推断对照显示边界条件：当答案可由局部信息恢复时，命名效应较小。

Conclusion: 结果并非说明标识符无用，而是揭示当前LLM在平衡词法线索与程序结构时存在系统性脆弱性。作者呼吁发展新的评测与建模方法，在保留自然代码规律收益的同时，使结论建立在准确、形式化的代码语义之上。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have made them widely used for code-related tasks.
