Identifier names are statistically informative in naturally occurring code, but their information is not always reliable. We investigate whether current LLMs assign disproportionate weight to lexical cues when renaming preserves program structure. We introduce Face/Off, a semantics-preserving identifier-renaming framework, and evaluate progressive naming conditions across multiple models and code-comprehension tasks. Within this framework, lexical overemphasis is pervasive across the evaluated models and primary tasks: performance generally decreases as identifier information is removed or made misleading, and outputs are often directed toward the meanings suggested by misleading names. The pattern persists under representative prompt- and fine-tuning-based interventions, suggesting that lexical overemphasis is an entrenched problem. A type-inference control confirms a boundary: naming effects are smaller when the answer is locally recoverable without the target name. These results do not imply that identifiers are unhelpful; rather, they reveal a systematic vulnerability in how current LLMs balance lexical cues against program structure. Our findings motivate evaluations and modeling methods that preserve the benefits of natural code regularities while keeping conclusions grounded in accurate, formalized code semantics.

</details>


### [240] [FeatLens: Feature-Guided Dynamic Code Graph Construction and Retrieval for Repository-Level Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26480)
*Xutian Li,Bo Xiong,Yifeng Zhu,Kunze Li,Xianlin Zhao,Runbang Yan,Yanzhen Zou,Lu Zhang,Bing Xie*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FeatLens：面向仓库级代码生成的特征引导动态代码图构建与检索方法，通过特征索引按需构建任务特定种子图，并用语义-结构图推理与个性化PageRank选出紧凑推理图，替代持久化全仓图和LLM探索，实现轻量确定性的依赖检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库级代码生成需LLM识别可复用的仓库依赖（函数、API、跨文件定义）。现有检索方法存在高成本问题：代码相似度检索缺乏结构性、持久化全仓图构建开销大、LLM驱动图探索推理与token成本高。特征导向方法虽贴合软件功能语义，但主要用于需求分解、规划或特征编辑，未用于代码依赖检索。

Method: 构建特征索引，将自然语言特征描述与函数级代码实体关联；给定生成任务后，基于特征索引动态构建任务特定种子图；在种子图上进行语义-结构图推理，并使用个性化PageRank选择紧凑推理图，以确定性、轻量方式完成依赖检索。

Result: 在DevEval与EvoCodeBench上DR@15达0.501和0.460，优于稀疏、稠密及图基线；DevEval生成任务DIR@1最高，DeepSeek-V3.2为52.91%、GPT-5-mini为53.58%，Pass@1具竞争力且生成代码更短。相比最强图基线，图节点减少61.0%，边减少86.2%，总token开销减少45.9%，检索阶段不消耗LLM token。

Conclusion: 特征引导的动态代码图构建与检索能有效替代持久化全仓图维护和LLM图探索，在提升依赖检索质量与生成指标的同时显著降低图规模与token成本，为仓库级代码生成提供高效方案。

Abstract: Recent code generation research has moved from isolated function completion toward repository-level generation in existing codebases. To implement a target function correctly, an LLM must identify reusable repository dependencies such as existing functions, APIs, and cross-file definitions. Existing retrieval methods provide such context through code similarity search, persistent whole-repository graphs, or LLM-driven graph exploration, but often incur high graph construction, reasoning, and token costs. Feature-oriented methods offer a natural view of software functionality, yet they mainly support requirement decomposition, planning, or feature editing rather than code dependency retrieval. This paper presents \textbf{FeatLens}, a feature-guided dynamic code graph construction and retrieval approach for repository-level code generation. FeatLens builds a feature index that links natural-language feature descriptions to function-level code entities. Given a generation task, it dynamically constructs a task-specific seed graph from the feature index and applies semantic-structural graph reasoning with personalized PageRank to select a compact reasoning graph. This design replaces persistent whole-repository graph maintenance and LLM exploration with deterministic and lightweight dependency retrieval. Experiments on DevEval and EvoCodeBench show that FeatLens achieves the best DR@15 among sparse, dense, and graph-based baselines (0.501 and 0.460). On DevEval generation, it obtains the highest DIR@1, reaching 52.91\% with DeepSeek-V3.2 and 53.58\% with GPT-5-mini, while maintaining competitive Pass@1 and producing shorter code. Compared with the strongest graph-based baseline, FeatLens reduces graph nodes by 61.0\%, edges by 86.2\%, and total token overhead by 45.9\%, with no LLM tokens used during retrieval.

</details>


### [241] [From Approval to Execution: Reconstruction-Aware Repair Analysis for LLM-Agent Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26529)
*Junchi Zhu,Zhenguang Liu,Shaojing Fan,Jianhai Chen,Qinming He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出"重建稳定授权"(ReSA)问题：审批环节展示的动作在重载、投影、参数重绑定与持久状态查询后可能被替换，导致"检查—使用"之间出现残余授权绕过；作者在APAS-Finder中实现分析并在独立sink预言机前冻结预测，28个受控结果全部吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体软件中审批机制是高风险动作的主要防线，但审批所见的动作往往不是最终被执行的对象。既有的字段流与检查覆盖分析只能证明"预期字段被检查过"，无法证明被检查的对象版本真正到达sink，也无法证明检查与使用之间没有替换发生，因此局部完整的修复仍可能留下残余授权绕过。

Method: 作者给出三项设计：(1) 在表示转换、sink依赖、被消费版本与授权范围之上形式化"重建稳定授权"(ReSA)；(2) 推导出一组义务，用于判定候选修复并暴露残余的sink后缀；(3) 在APAS-Finder中实现该分析，并在独立sink预言机观察执行之前冻结预测。

Result: 预测与全部28个受控结果一致。四对匹配样本在字段流与检查覆盖完全相同的情况下却要求相反的判定；在补充对象流、支配关系、干扰与相同授权契约后，一个CodeQL组合分析可全部还原这八例。两名分析师在四例模型验证定性上完全一致，在sink依赖上一致19/20。在已发布消费者上，五项修复阻止了60/60的范围外效应，三项机制对比则暴露出预测的残余效应。

Conclusion: 修复的充分性取决于被实际消费的重建后动作，而非审批记录或更早被检查的表示。仅凭字段流与检查覆盖无法保证授权安全，必须建模对象版本的实际到达与检查—使用间的替换。

Abstract: Approval mechanisms have become a primary safeguard for consequential actions in LLM-agent software. Yet the action shown for approval is often not the object ultimately consumed: workflow reload, transcript projection, argument rebinding, and durable-state lookup may reconstruct it before execution. Existing fieldflow and check-coverage analyses can establish that expected fields were inspected, but not that the inspected object version reaches the sink or that no replacement intervenes between check and use. Consequently, a locally complete repair may still leave a residual authorization bypass after reconstruction. We address this problem through three designs. (1) We formulate reconstruction-stable authorization (ReSA) over representation transitions, sink dependencies, consumed versions, and grant scope. (2) We derive obligations that judge candidate repairs and expose residual sink suffixes. (3) We implement the analysis in APAS-Finder and freeze predictions before an independent sink oracle observes execution. Predictions agree with all 28 controlled outcomes. Four matched pairs require opposite judgments despite identical field-flow and check coverage; a CodeQL composite recovers all eight when supplied object flow, dominance, interference, and the same grant contract. Two analysts agree on all four model-validation dispositions and 19/20 sink dependencies. On released consumers, five repairs prevent 60/60 tested out-of-scope effects, while three mechanism contrasts expose the predicted residual effects. Repair sufficiency therefore depends on the reconstructed action consumed, not merely on an approval record or earlier checked representation.

</details>


### [242] [TraceVIC: Causal Reasoning over Code Evolution for Identifying Vulnerability-Inducing Commits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26711)
*Fnu Tanish,Samiha Shimmi,Samikshya Chapagain,Hamed Okhravi,Mona Rahimi,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TraceVIC：基于时序图推理代码演化，定位并排序漏洞引入提交（VIC），F2 由0.637提升至0.814，较SOTA最高提升28.7%，在4个未见C/C++项目中79个漏洞有78个找到有效VIC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件漏洞常在引入很久后才被发现，难以确定引入脆弱条件的VIC。现有技术主要依赖git blame沿修订历史追溯脆弱代码，并采用位置启发式（选最早或最近的修改），但真实VIC可能位于历史任意位置，且脆弱行为可能依赖跨多次修订演化的代码。因此VIC识别需要对漏洞相关代码如何演化进行推理，而非仅看候选提交在历史中的位置。

Method: TraceVIC采用基于时序图的方法，通过推理代码演化来识别和排序VIC。首先定位可能的根因行，并跨修订追溯其历史，构建图表示以刻画每次修订内的程序结构及漏洞相关代码在历史中的演化；随后对得到的修订历史进行推理，用时序边保持相邻修订间程序元素的对应关系，并依据候选提交对脆弱条件的贡献直接排序。

Result: 消融实验表明，建模完整修订历史使F2由0.637提升至0.814。TraceVIC较最先进方法F2最高提升28.7%，在4个未见C/C++项目的79个漏洞中为78个识别出有效VIC。

Conclusion: VIC识别应建模漏洞相关代码的演化而非依赖历史位置启发式；TraceVIC用时序图推理完整修订历史，显著提升识别效果与泛化能力。

Abstract: Software vulnerabilities are often discovered long after they are introduced, making it difficult to identify the vulnerability-inducing commit (VIC) responsible for introducing the underlying vulnerable condition. Existing VIC identification techniques largely rely on git blame to trace vulnerable code through revision history and use positional heuristics, such as selecting its earliest or most recent modification. However, the true VIC may occur anywhere within this history, and vulnerable behavior may depend on code that evolves across multiple revisions. We therefore argue that VIC identification requires reasoning about how vulnerability-relevant code evolves, rather than simply where a candidate commit appears in the revision history.
  We present TraceVIC, a temporal graph-based approach for identifying and ranking VICs by reasoning over code evolution. TraceVIC first localizes likely root-cause lines and traces their histories across revisions, constructing graph representations that capture program structure within each revision and the evolution of vulnerability-relevant code across the history. It reasons over the resulting revision history, using temporal edges to preserve correspondences between program elements across consecutive revisions, and directly ranks candidate commits according to their contribution to the vulnerable condition.
  Ablation results show that modeling the full revision history improves F2 from 0.637 to 0.814. TraceVIC improves F2 by up to 28.7% over state-of-the-art methods and identifies a valid VIC for 78 of 79 vulnerabilities across four unseen C/C++ projects.

</details>


### [243] [Metrics Failure in LLM-Based Code Vulnerability Repair: An Empirical Study and a Change-Aware Screen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26749)
*Om Nepal,Sushant Aryal,Oluseyi Olukola,Nick Rahimi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过五项受控实验（203个Big-Vul漏洞函数、3个开源代码LLM、3种提示策略）论证：编译率不能作为单函数漏洞修复的可靠指标，并建议采用变化感知、基于执行的评估；提出的diff_F1可作为廉价预筛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM自动化修复C/C++安全漏洞的研究中，编译率（生成补丁是否可编译）被普遍当作进展的代理指标，但作者认为该指标在单函数漏洞修复场景中科学上不可靠，需要系统性地验证其缺陷并寻找替代方案。

Method: 在Big-Vul的203个漏洞函数上，使用3个开源代码LLM（350M–6.7B参数）和3种提示策略开展五项受控实验：检验编译率对有效干预的敏感性、分解编译失败的归因（模型 vs 评估框架/数据集伪影）、测试单一编译器标准标志的影响、与参考相似度指标的模型排序对比、以及将编译率作为优化目标（编译器反馈循环）时的行为；同时检验全函数CodeBLEU的退化表现，并提出仅对编辑区域打分的变化感知指标diff_F1。

Result: 编译率(i)对显著改善生成代码的干预几乎无响应；(ii)主要受评估框架与数据集伪影支配，约64%的编译失败不归因于模型，且该比例在各模型间近乎不变；(iii)在单一编译器标准标志下，相同补丁的编译率波动达1.8–2.7倍且无回归；(iv)对三个模型的排序与参考相似度指标完全相反；(v)作为优化目标会奖励非修复，编译器反馈循环提高编译率的同时降低与人工修复的相似度，人工检查发现新编译输出中存在删除式和占位符式非修复。全函数CodeBLEU同样失效：漏洞输入的未修改副本得分高于所有模型。diff_F1对无操作补丁给零分，对部分（非全部）删除式作弊补丁接近零分，同时仍能给真正的部分编辑以分数。

Conclusion: 编译率及全函数CodeBLEU均不适合作为单函数漏洞修复的修复质量指标，作者主张采用变化感知、基于执行的评估方法；diff_F1可作为深入执行分析前的廉价预筛，但它本身并非修复质量指标，作者亦报告了其局限。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to the automated repair of C/C++ security vulnerabilities, and compile rate is a commonly reported proxy for progress: whether the generated patch compiles. We argue that compile rate is a scientifically unreliable metric for single-function vulnerability repair, and we support this with five controlled experiments over 203 vulnerable functions from Big-Vul, three open-source code LLMs (350M to 6.7B parameters), and three prompting strategies. Compile rate (i) barely responds to an intervention that substantially improves the generated code; (ii) is dominated by evaluation-harness and dataset artifacts rather than model quality, with about 64% of compile failures not attributable to the model, a share that is nearly invariant across models; (iii) shifts by 1.8 to 2.7 times on identical patches under a single compiler-standard flag, with zero regressions; (iv) ranks the three models in the opposite order to reference-similarity metrics; and (v) rewards non-repairs when used as an optimization target, since a compiler-feedback loop raises compile rate while similarity to the human fix falls, with manual inspection finding deletion- and placeholder-style non-repairs among the newly compiling outputs. The natural fallback, whole-function CodeBLEU, also fails: an unchanged copy of the vulnerable input outscores every model. We also examine diff_F1, a change-aware screen that scores only the edited region. It gives exactly zero credit to a no-op and near-zero credit to some, though not all, of the deletion-based gaming patches we observed, while still crediting genuine partial edits, so it may serve as a cheap screen before deeper, execution-based analysis. It is not a repair-quality metric, and we report where it falls short. Our findings argue for change-aware, execution-grounded evaluation of LLM-based vulnerability repair.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [244] [GroundedGEO: Auditing the Evidence Gap in Generative Search Rankings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25189)
*Yihan Xia,Huiling Fan,Kangrong Zhong,Taotao Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文通过证据配对基准（50个电商查询、1950个案例）与claim级重排器GroundedGEO，审计生成式搜索中"文本相关但缺乏证据支持"的可识别性缺口：无支持丰富变体在Qwen2.5-7B上获得显著排名增益（+0.065~+0.092），该效应依赖模型；oracle证据标签可将无支持丰富top-3率从0.65降至0.43且零误抑制，但自动评判器均未通过预注册可靠性门槛，揭示证据通道受标签质量与packet覆盖两重限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式搜索系统为高风险决策排序产品与服务，发布者可廉价地让候选文本显得相关。然而证据状态并非文本属性，而是"主张—证据"关系：纯文本排序器与防御手段无法区分诚实的详细内容与伪造的细节，由此形成可识别性缺口（identifiability gap）。作者旨在审计这一缺口，并评估证据通道作为防御机制的有效边界。

Method: 构建证据配对基准（50个电商查询，1950个案例）与claim级重排器GroundedGEO，对供给packet中缺乏支持的查询相关主张施加惩罚。设计受控对照：匹配的丰富变体控制格式与体量；packet twins在文本固定下增补证词（attestation）；thinned packets则撤回证词。在冻结的listwise排序器Qwen2.5-7B、MiMo-v2.5、GLM-5.3-Flash及pointwise scorer上评估，并以370条人工金标注检验自动评判器。

Result: 在冻结的listwise排序器Qwen2.5-7B上，无支持丰富变体相对干净候选显示显著归一化排名增益（各claim profile下+0.065~+0.092，Holm校正），而受支持与中性对照无此效应；该效应具模型依赖性（MiMo-v2.5上边缘，GLM-5.3-Flash上缺失）。在冻结pointwise scorer上，oracle证据标签在lambda=40时将无支持丰富top-3率从0.65降至0.43（laundering从0.61降至0.39），零误抑制；packet twins在文本不变下恢复原始比率。针对370条人工金标注，所有测试的自动评判器均未通过预注册可靠性门槛，但最佳本地评判器在受保护臂上保留了79-100%的oracle抑制且测得零误抑制。单独剥离证词覆盖会使误抑制增加0.307。

Conclusion: 这些诊断性效应识别出证据通道的两项限制：标签质量（label quality）与packet覆盖（packet coverage）。研究并未验证某自动防御方案的有效性，且对不利人工金标注臂的解读仍待裁决（pending adjudication）。

Abstract: Generative search systems rank products and services for consequential decisions, and publishers can cheaply make candidate text look relevant. Yet evidence status is not a text property but a claim-evidence relation: text-only rankers and defenses cannot separate honest detailed content from fabricated detail, creating an identifiability gap. We audit this gap with an evidence-paired benchmark (50 e-commerce queries, 1,950 cases) and a claim-level reranker, GroundedGEO, that penalizes query-relevant claims lacking support in a supplied packet. Matched rich variants control format and volume; packet twins add attestations at fixed text, while thinned packets withdraw them. On the frozen listwise ranker Qwen2.5-7B, unsupported-rich variants show significant normalized rank gain over clean candidates (+0.065 to +0.092 across claim profiles, Holm-corrected), while supported and neutral controls do not; the effect is model-dependent (marginal on MiMo-v2.5, absent on GLM-5.3-Flash). On a frozen pointwise scorer, oracle evidence labels cut the unsupported-rich top-3 rate from 0.65 to 0.43 (laundering from 0.61 to 0.39) at lambda=40 with zero false suppression; packet twins restore the original rates without changing text. Against a 370-claim human gold, all tested automatic judges fail the preregistered reliability gate, although the best local judge retains 79-100% of oracle suppression with zero measured false suppression on protected arms. Separately, stripping attestation coverage increases false suppression by 0.307. These diagnostic effects identify two limits on the evidence channel: label quality and packet coverage. They do not validate an automatic defense, and interpretation of the adverse human-gold arm remains pending adjudication.

</details>


### [245] [ReFilter: Bridging Embeddings and LLM Filtering for Similar Mobile App Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25306)
*Buthayna AlMulla,Maram Assi,Safwat Hassan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出ReFilter混合框架，先用嵌入检索语义相关候选应用，再用LLM上下文过滤识别功能相似应用，F1达90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相似移动应用检索对研究者（生态与趋势研究）、开发者（竞品分析）和终端用户（应用推荐）都很重要。现有方法依赖嵌入检索，能捕捉语义相似性但无法识别功能相似的应用；而直接用LLM过滤虽有潜力，却因需评估大量应用对而计算成本过高，此前无人采用。

Method: ReFilter采用两阶段混合框架：第一阶段用嵌入模型检索语义相关的候选应用，缩小搜索空间；第二阶段对这些候选对施加基于LLM的上下文过滤，以更高精度判定真正的功能相似应用，从而在效率与准确率间取得平衡。

Result: 在相似应用检索任务上取得90%的F1分数。

Conclusion: ReFilter提升了应用替代方案的相关性，可支持更准确的应用对比，并有助于改进生态系统理解、竞品分析和推荐。

Abstract: Retrieving similar mobile applications (apps) is essential for researchers, developers, and end-users. Researchers use similarity detection to study app ecosystems and trends, developers for competitor analysis, and end-users for focused app recommendations. Existing approaches rely on embedding-based retrieval, which captures semantic similarity but fails to identify functionally similar apps. To our knowledge, no prior work has applied large language model (LLM)-based filtering to this task, due to the high computational cost of evaluating large numbers of app pairs. To address this gap, we propose ReFilter, a hybrid framework that first Retrieves semantically related candidate apps using embeddings and then applies LLM-based contextual Filtering to identify true functionally similar apps with higher precision. This design balances efficiency and accuracy, achieving an F1-score of 90% for retrieving similar apps. By improving the relevance of app alternatives, ReFilter enables more accurate app comparisons and supports improved ecosystem understanding, competitor analysis, and recommendations.

</details>


### [246] [Robust Fusion of Semantic and Behavioural Signals for LLM Reranking in Personalised Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25825)
*Aleksandr V. Petrov,Nathan Stein,Erik Lybecker,Emma Schüldt,Daniel Lazarovski,Hugues Bouchard,Mounia Lalmas*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 在LLM跨编码器重排序中注入行为统计特征（QSS）虽提升排序质量，但会引发捷径学习，导致特征缺失时鲁棒性下降；作者提出确定性双样本特征dropout训练，每个样本分别以含QSS和去QSS两种形式呈现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化搜索需兼顾查询意图与用户上下文、历史交互。LLM跨编码器可用统一接口做重排序，但向提示中注入预测性行为统计会诱发捷径学习：模型过度依赖历史信号，牺牲可泛化到稀疏或未见查询的语义与用户上下文模式。

Method: 在大规模音频流媒体平台的个性化搜索系统中，以Query Slice Stats（QSS）——一种由交互推导、总结查询-候选对历史成功情况的行为特征——为研究对象。针对朴素QSS注入的缺陷，提出确定性双样本特征dropout训练：每个训练样本分别以包含QSS和移除QSS两种形式各呈现一次。

Result: 离线实验中，QSS注入在特征可用时将排序质量提升13.3%；双样本训练在保持这些增益的同时，在去QSS评估下相较朴素QSS训练相对提升4.0%。在线A/B测试中，两种QSS感知变体均使搜索成功率提升约2%；总体测试未能区分双样本与仅特征训练，冷启动对比结果与离线结论方向一致。

Conclusion: 成对的特征-有/特征-无训练可缓解利用强行为统计与在其不可用时保持鲁棒性之间的张力。

Abstract: Personalised search must satisfy query intent while incorporating user context and historical interactions. LLM-based cross-encoders provide a single reranking interface, but injecting predictive behavioural statistics into their prompts can encourage shortcut learning: reliance on historical signals at the expense of semantic and user-context patterns that generalise to sparse or unseen searches.
  We study this problem in the personalised search system of a large-scale audio streaming platform using Query Slice Stats (QSS), an interaction-derived behavioural feature summarising historical success for query-candidate pairs. Naive QSS injection improves ranking when the feature is available but reduces robustness when it is removed. We address this with deterministic dual-sample feature-dropout training, which presents each example once with QSS included and once with QSS removed.
  Offline, QSS injection improves ranking quality by 13.3% when available. Dual-sample training preserves these gains while improving performance under QSS-removed evaluation by 4.0% relative to naive QSS training. In a live online test, both QSS-aware variants improve search success by roughly 2%. The aggregate test does not distinguish dual-sample from features-only training; the cold-start comparison is directionally consistent with the offline results.
  Paired feature-present and feature-removed training can therefore reduce the tension between exploiting strong behavioural statistics and remaining robust when they are unavailable.

</details>


### [247] [Knowledge-as-Skill: A Structural Design for Autonomous Knowledge-Base Use by LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25991)
*Jiangxu Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 Knowledge-as-Skill 知识组织方案，将知识库结构化为可发现、可导航、自描述的三层体系（SKILL.md 发现层、index.md 导航层、带 YAML frontmatter 的知识层），并给出异构文档转换流水线；在 WixQA 基准上 Factuality 与 Context Recall 优于 Corpus2Skill。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG 的"检索-拼接-生成"流水线由系统替模型做检索决策；随着工具调用与智能体循环趋于可靠，智能体可自主决定是否检索、检索什么、何时停止，但由此暴露出新瓶颈：智能体不了解知识库的内容范围、用途、来源与关系，传统知识库仅将文档以匿名文本块形式暴露。

Method: Knowledge-as-Skill 组织方案，含三层：以 SKILL.md 为核心的发现层、每个目录一个 index.md 的导航层、以及含 topic/type/provenance/lifecycle 等 YAML frontmatter 的知识层；设计遵循 Open Knowledge Format (OKF) 与 Skill 协议，不修改智能体框架；并提供 knowledge-as-skill 流水线，将 PDF、Word、网页导出、笔记等异构集合转换为该结构。

Result: 在 WixQA 企业客服基准的初步评测中，Factuality 0.889、Context Recall 0.816，对比 Corpus2Skill 报告的 0.767 与 0.708；Faithfulness 略低、Context Precision 更低、交互轮次更多。作者声明因模型、提示词与知识包构建方式不同，属方向性跨工作证据而非受控对比。

Conclusion: 通过让知识库自描述、可发现、可导航，可提升智能体自主检索下的答案事实性与上下文召回；但需在忠实度、上下文精确率与交互成本间权衡，且结论仍需受控实验验证。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) gives large language models (LLMs) access to external knowledge, but its conventional retrieve-concatenate-generate pipeline makes retrieval decisions on behalf of the model. As tool use and agent loops become more reliable, an agent can decide whether to retrieve, what to inspect, and when to stop. This shift exposes a new bottleneck: the agent may not know what a knowledge base contains. Traditional knowledge bases expose documents as anonymous text chunks with limited information about scope, purpose, provenance, or relations.
  We propose Knowledge-as-Skill, an organization scheme that makes a knowledge base discoverable, navigable, and self-descriptive. It has three layers: a discovery layer centered on SKILL.md; a navigation layer with one index.md per directory; and a knowledge layer containing documents with YAML frontmatter for topic, type, provenance, and lifecycle. The design follows the Open Knowledge Format (OKF) and the Skill protocol without modifying the agent framework. We also provide knowledge-as-skill, a pipeline for converting heterogeneous collections of PDFs, Word files, web exports, and notes into this structure.
  In a preliminary evaluation on the WixQA enterprise customer-support benchmark, our setup obtains 0.889 Factuality and 0.816 Context Recall, compared with reported Corpus2Skill values of 0.767 and 0.708. It obtains slightly lower Faithfulness, lower Context Precision, and more interaction turns. Because the models, prompts, and knowledge-package construction differ, these results are directional cross-work evidence rather than a controlled comparison.

</details>


### [248] [TailSpec-EASE: Knowledge-Graph-Regularized Linear Recommendation for Web Long-Tail Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26143)
*Jianru Shen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TailSpec-EASE 将关系感知的谱知识图谱先验注入局部闭式重构目标，先验强度随物品流行度自适应，从而在不牺牲整体精度的前提下显著提升长尾推荐效果并大幅降低训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web 平台推荐系统过度服务热门物品、忽视长尾物品。物品侧知识图谱（常以关联数据或 RDF 形式在 Web 上提供）可通过共享语义属性连接稀疏物品来缓解该问题，但多数知识图谱感知推荐器依赖图神经网络架构，计算昂贵；而 EASE-R 等强浅层线性模型忽略侧信息，其全局闭式版本在大规模目录上不可行。

Method: 提出 TailSpec-EASE：一种轻量级推荐器，将关系感知的谱知识图谱先验注入局部闭式重构目标；先验强度根据物品流行度自适应调整，为长尾物品提供更强的语义引导。

Result: 在四个公开基准上，与经典、线性、图协同过滤、知识图谱感知神经及分数级知识图谱基线相比，在整体精度、长尾性能与训练成本间取得良好折中；相比无知识图谱版本，NDCG@20 最高提升 24%。所有长尾指标的提升在成对 bootstrap 下统计显著，四个数据集中有三个整体 NDCG 显著提升。在 Amazon-book 上，TailSpec-EASE 于 CPU 上训练仅需 37 秒，而 KGAT（GPU）需 2,584 秒、LightGCN（CPU）需 15,800 秒，且其 NDCG@20 与 Tail Recall@20 更高；在全局闭式模型内存溢出的目录上仍可运行。

Conclusion: TailSpec-EASE 以轻量线性框架融合知识图谱语义先验，兼顾整体精度、长尾表现与训练效率，在可扩展性与统计显著性上均优于现有基线。

Abstract: Recommender systems on Web platforms tend to over-serve popular items and neglect the long tail. Item-side knowledge graphs (KGs), often available as linked data or RDF-style Web resources, can help by connecting sparse items through shared semantic attributes. Many competitive KG-aware recommenders rely on graph neural architectures, whereas strong shallow linear models such as EASE-R typically ignore side information and may become infeasible in their global closed-form version. We introduce TailSpec-EASE, a lightweight recommender that injects a relation-aware spectral KG prior into a local closed-form reconstruction objective. The prior strength adapts to item popularity, giving stronger semantic guidance to long-tail items. Across four public benchmarks and a broad set of classical, linear, graph-CF, KG-aware neural, and score-level KG baselines, TailSpec-EASE attains a favorable trade-off between overall accuracy, long-tail performance, and training cost. It improves NDCG@20 by up to 24% over its counterpart without KG information. All tail-metric improvements over the no-KG counterpart are statistically significant under a paired bootstrap, and overall NDCG improves significantly on three of the four datasets. In a representative Amazon-book timing study, TailSpec-EASE trains in 37 seconds on CPU, compared with 2,584 seconds for a GPU-trained KGAT run and 15,800 seconds for CPU LightGCN, while attaining higher NDCG@20 and Tail Recall@20 on that dataset. It also remains feasible on catalogs where the global closed-form model runs out of memory.

</details>


### [249] [When Concealed Links Cannot Be Recovered: A Structural Identifiability Bound and Evaluation Pitfalls in Offshore Leak Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26171)
*Joseph Bingham*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个无分布的可辨识性下界，证明在尊重图同构的任意恢复规则下，隐藏的受益所有权边在结构不可区分时无法高于随机猜测恢复，并用ICIJ离岸泄密图实证了隔离节点恢复率恰为0.5的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巴拿马文件、天堂文件等泄密揭示离岸实体网络，自然引出一个网络学习问题：这些结构刻意隐藏的关系（尤其是谁实际拥有什么）能否通过链接预测从泄密的公开部分恢复？作者认为答案基本是否定的，现有声称可以恢复的分析测量了错误的对象，需要澄清并给出理论界限。

Method: 提出无分布的可辨识性界限：对任何尊重图同构的恢复规则（涵盖所有拓扑链接预测评分与所有消息传递图神经网络），在观测图中处于孤立的隐藏端点与其结构孪生节点不可区分，隐藏边无法高于随机恢复。一般而言恢复上限由节点的结构不可区分类大小决定，Weisfeiler-Leman颜色类是可计算的替代指标，节点度仅是粗略代理。在完整ICIJ离岸泄密图（814K实体、84K标注受益所有人边）上使用无分类器、度控制的探针，并用训练好的图神经网络验证。

Result: 无分类器、度控制探针在孤立所有者上复现精确的0.5下限，孤立所有者占全部所有者的20.6%；训练好的图神经网络落在同一下限。恢复率仅随结构独特性上升而提高，且该下限在五个泄密数据集中均重现。同时记录了五种评估陷阱，每种都会让本不可突破的界限看似可被突破，并给出规避规则。

Conclusion: 实际结论是应将研究重心从近乎不可恢复的隐藏委托人转向隐藏机制本身，并说明融合外部数据的帮助远低于预期。

Abstract: Leaks such as the Panama and Paradise Papers expose large networks of offshore entities, and they invite an obvious question for network learning. Can the relations these structures are built to hide---above all, who beneficially owns what---be recovered from the public part of the leak by link prediction? We argue that the answer is mostly no, and that the analyses which suggest otherwise are measuring the wrong thing. Our main result is a distribution-free identifiability bound. For any recovery rule that respects graph isomorphism, and that covers every topological link-prediction score together with every message-passing graph neural network, a concealed endpoint left isolated in the observed graph is interchangeable with its structural twins, so its hidden edge cannot be recovered above chance. Isolation is only the sharpest case. In general the ceiling on recovery is set by the size of a node's structural-indistinguishability class, for which the Weisfeiler--Leman colour class is a computable stand-in, and node degree is at best a loose proxy. On the full ICIJ Offshore Leaks graph (814K entities and 84K labelled beneficial-owner edges) a classifier-free, degree-controlled probe reproduces an exact $0.5$ floor for isolated owners, who make up $20.6\%$ of all owners, and a trained graph neural network lands on the same floor. Recovery climbs only as structural distinctiveness grows, and the floor reappears in every one of the five leaks. Along the way we document five evaluation traps. Each one makes a bound that cannot be beaten look beaten, and we give a short rule that avoids them. The practical upshot is to redirect effort from the hidden principal, which is close to unrecoverable, toward the machinery of concealment, and to spell out why fusing external data helps far less than one would hope.

</details>


### [250] [A Semantic Approach to the Academic Publishing Network: Document Vector Representations and Hybrid Structural-Semantic Fusion over OpenAlex Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26218)
*Robert Šamárek,Radek Martinek*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文在学术出版网络的结构图分析基础上引入语义层与可调参数的"结构-语义"融合机制：用SPECTER2引文感知嵌入表示文献并存入以OpenAlex ID为键的向量库，与图结构层直接对接；采用模块化晚期融合函数，以权重α线性组合语义相似度（嵌入余弦）与结构相似度（书目耦合）。在VSB-奥斯特拉瓦技术大学语料上验证：引文嵌入比TF-IDF基线更符合OpenAlex专家主题分类；在推荐任务中，结构、语义及融合信号在不同数据条件下各有优势。融合并非普适最优，而是按任务调度的互补信号机制。方法以开源apnet库扩展形式发布，流程可复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构图分析能刻画学术出版网络中实体间的拓扑关系，但看不到论文内容本身，只依赖引用结构会丢失语义信息。作者希望在不放弃结构视角的前提下弥补这一缺口，通过在结构层之上叠加语义层并引入可调融合参数，使两类互补信号能够根据具体任务与可用数据灵活组合。

Method: 1）语义表示：用引文感知的SPECTER2向量嵌入表示科学文档；2）存储与对齐：嵌入存入嵌入式向量数据库，以稳定的OpenAlex ID为主键，实现与图层的直接连接；3）融合函数：定义模块化晚期融合，将语义相似度（嵌入余弦）与结构相似度（书目耦合）以可调权重α组合；4）α取值按具体任务选定；5）在VSB-奥斯特拉瓦技术大学语料上开展主题一致性与推荐用例评估；6）以开源apnet库扩展形式发布完整可复现工作流。

Result: 在VSB-奥斯特拉瓦技术大学语料上得到两点结论：（1）引文感知嵌入与OpenAlex专家主题分类体系的一致性优于TF-IDF基线；（2）在推荐用例中，结构信号、语义信号及二者组合信号在不同数据可用条件下各自进入优势区，即不同信号在不同regime下携带有效信息。

Conclusion: 混合融合并非普遍更优的方法，而是一种按任务显式调度互补信号的机制；结构、语义与融合信号的选择应依据任务与可用数据。作者将整套方法作为apnet开源库的扩展发布，并提供可复现工作流。

Abstract: Structural graph analysis of the academic publishing network captures the topological relationships between entities but does not see the content of works. Building on our structural approach, this work complements it with a semantic layer and a parameterized structural-semantic fusion. We represent scientific documents by citation-informed vector embeddings (SPECTER2) and store them in an embedded vector database keyed by the stable OpenAlex ID, so that they connect directly to the graph layer. We define a modular late-fusion function that combines semantic similarity (cosine of embeddings) and structural similarity (bibliographic coupling) with a tunable weight alpha whose value is chosen according to the specific task. On the corpus of VSB - Technical University of Ostrava we show two things: citation-informed embeddings agree with the expert OpenAlex topical taxonomy better than a TF-IDF baseline, and in a recommendation use case the structural, semantic, and combined signals carry information in different regimes depending on the available data. Hybrid fusion here is not a universally better method but an explicit mechanism for steering complementary signals according to the task. We release the whole approach as an open-source extension of the apnet library with a reproducible workflow.

</details>


### [251] [ABAI at COLIEE 2026 Task 1: Multi-Stage Retrieval with GraphRAG-Enhanced Meta-Learning, and a Post-Hoc Study of the Cross-Validation-to-Test Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26237)
*Minhan Cho,Soyoung Park,Daejin Choi,Jinyoung Han*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文报告了 ABAI 团队参加 COLIEE 2026 Task 1（判例检索）的提交系统及对其表现不佳原因的受控分析，最终将失败归因于召回到顶而非阈值或分布漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该任务抑制了被引段落本身，去除了检索器通常依赖的大量词汇重叠信号，导致检索难度显著上升；作者希望不仅提交系统，还要通过受控实验查清性能差距的真实来源。

Method: 四阶段独立训练的流水线：基于引用上下文窗口的多视图 BM25 与倒数排名融合（RRF）、神经重排序、基于实体社区与图注意力网络（GAT）的图特征、以及在 34 维特征上的 LightGBM 元学习器。随后对三类失败假设（召回天花板、时间分布漂移、阈值失校）进行拆分验证，并测度各半区补救措施（BM25 长度归一化调参、事件三元组视图、全内容稠密融合、引文图特征、逐查询截断规则、零样本重排序器替换、日期过滤）。

Result: 最佳运行在官方测试集上 F1=0.177，而交叉验证为 0.311。无泄漏协议下阈值迁移仅损失 0.007 F1；决策质量在各时间四分位间持平；官方测试查询在两种独立嵌入空间中并未比训练查询彼此之间更远离训练流形。漏检在两半区间均等分布：从未召回的候选与召回但排名低于截断的候选各占一半。BM25 长度归一化调参、事件三元组视图与全内容稠密融合将 top-200 召回提升 3–7 个百分点；去除自引泄漏后引文图特征在 8 个随机种子上提升 0.014 F1；逐查询截断规则、零样本重排序器替换与日期过滤均无增益。另记录了四个评估伪影，每个在协议修正后都曾反转结论。

Conclusion: 性能差距并非源于阈值迁移或时间分布漂移，而是召回瓶颈与排序截断共同造成的等分漏检；提升 top-200 召回与使用引文图特征有效，而阈值类与零样本替换类补救无效。作者强调严格的去泄漏评估协议至关重要，因为评估伪影足以反转结论。

Abstract: We present the ABAI submission to COLIEE 2026 Task 1, case law retrieval, together with a controlled study of why it underperformed. The task suppresses the cited passages themselves, which removes much of the lexical overlap a retriever would rely on. Our pipeline answers this with four independently trained stages: multi-view BM25 over citation-context windows with reciprocal rank fusion, neural reranking, graph-based features from entity communities and a graph attention network, and a LightGBM meta-learner over 34 features. Our best run reached F1=0.177 on the official test set, against a cross-validated 0.311, and we attributed that gap to a recall ceiling, temporal distribution shift, and threshold miscalibration. We then tested all three. Under leakage-free protocols threshold transfer costs 0.007 F1, decision quality is flat across chronological quartiles, and the official test queries are not measurably farther from the training manifold than training queries are from each other, in two independent embedding spaces. Decomposing the misses instead splits them exactly evenly between candidates never retrieved and candidates retrieved but ranked below the cut. Measuring the remedies for each half, BM25 length-normalisation tuning, an event-triple view, and full-content dense fusion lift top-200 recall by three to seven points, and citation-graph features add 0.014 F1 over eight seeds once own-citation leakage is removed, while per-query cutoff rules, a zero-shot reranker swap, and a date filter do not help. We also document four evaluation artifacts, each of which reversed a result once the protocol was corrected.

</details>


### [252] [Which Reranking Conclusions Survive the Answer Interface? A Prospective Finite-Orbit Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26250)
*Wenzhang Du*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文前瞻性审计了仅改变阅读器答案界面时，BM25与BGE-v2-m3的检索策略效应、排序及选择价值是否变化。六个确证设置均未出现超过0.015阈值的界面变异，也无BM25-BGE排序反转；但五个设置仍过于不确定，无法满足高阶等价条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重排序器日益通过下游语言模型答案进行评估，这引出一个检索测量问题：若仅改变阅读器的答案接口，对BM25与BGE-v2-m3是否会得出相同结论。作者希望厘清界面变化是否影响检索策略效应的估计、排序及选择价值。

Method: 在RAGuard与FEVER上、使用四个阅读器，固定检索策略、证据、声明与上下文深度，仅改变语义到标签绑定、A/B与X/Y词汇、选项顺序，构成八个任务等价界面，进行前瞻性配对效应审计，并检验界面变异、排序反转与选择价值差异。

Result: 六个确证设置中，界面变异均未超过预设0.015实质性阈值，也无BM25-BGE排序的认证反转。某一环境中选择器分歧达33.5%，但八个环境均未确立预设的实质性留出价值差异。五个设置仍过于不确定，无法满足预设高阶等价条件。

Conclusion: 结果不支持广泛的复现性不稳定，但也未证明普遍不变性。检索评估应分离策略层面、界面稳定性、策略排序与选择价值。当稳定性未经验证时，宜在枚举界面上取均匀平均并给出显式变异界限，以避免偏袒某一界面。

Abstract: Rerankers are increasingly evaluated through downstream language-model answers. This raises a retrieval-measurement question: if only the reader's answer interface changes, should we reach the same conclusion about BM25 versus BGE-v2-m3? We prospectively audit their claim-paired effect on RAGuard and FEVER with four readers. Retrieval policies, evidence, claims, and context depth remain fixed while semantic-to-label binding, A/B versus X/Y vocabulary, and option order form eight task-equivalent interfaces. We ask whether the estimated retrieval-policy effect, its ordering, or selection value changes. None of the six confirmatory settings showed statistically certified interface variation above the prespecified 0.015 materiality threshold, and none showed a certified reversal of the BM25-BGE ordering. Selector disagreement reaches 33.5% in one environment, yet none of eight environments establishes the prespecified material held-out value difference. These results do not support broad replicated instability, but they do not prove universal invariance: five settings remain too uncertain to satisfy the prespecified higher-order equivalence condition. They show why retrieval evaluations should separate policy level, interface stability, policy ordering, and selection value. When stability is unverified, a uniform average over the enumerated interfaces with explicit variation bounds avoids privileging one interface.

</details>


### [253] [When Does Permutation Instability Generalize? Independent-View Validation for Listwise LLM Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26251)
*Wenzhang Du*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 验证目标与探针共享测量会诱发部分-整体关联，从而夸大有限视图不稳定性分数的预测力；只有观测不相交的目标才能分离对未见排列的预测信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型列表式重排器在等价候选排列下常出现分歧，现有工作用有限不稳定性诊断来支持额外采样、聚合或选择性计算。但若验证统计量复用了探针视图，其与不稳定性分数的关联可能并非真实的预测信息，而是共享测量引起的经典部分-整体关联，因此需要澄清这类论断的可靠性。

Method: 推导精确的有限视图分解，前瞻性比较零个、一个、两个复用视图以及完全不相交的四视图目标。实验覆盖两个固定的7B模型家族和两个推荐数据集，使用受控列表进行有符号离线分析，并用未经处理的检索器列表进行无目标复制。

Result: 在四个受控块中，完全不相交的相关系数较弱或异质（-0.061至0.281），而复用两个探针视图则为0.600至0.718；所有配对对比差异较大（0.436至0.661）且经Holm校正显著。重叠效应在四个未处理列表块中均为正。探针从两视图增至四视图仅在一个块中明显提升不相交可靠性。探针能预测聚合移动幅度（Spearman rho=0.142至0.426），但不能预测稳定的有符号目标收益，且12个固定比例探针路由点中有7个在实测成本下被严格支配。

Conclusion: 当目标估计量是关于未见扰动行为的预测信息时，验证目标必须与探针在观测上不相交，才能分离该信息；有符号效用与成本敏感决策属于另外的问题。

Abstract: Listwise language-model rerankers often disagree across equivalent candidate permutations. Finite instability diagnostics are therefore used to motivate additional sampling, aggregation, or selective computation. But an association with a validation statistic that reuses the probe views need not isolate predictive information about unseen permutations. Shared measurements can induce classical part-whole association. We study how this affects claims that a finite-view instability score predicts unseen permutations. We derive the exact finite-view decomposition and prospectively compare zero, one, and two reused views, including a fully disjoint four-view target.
  The study covers two pinned 7B model families and two recommendation datasets, with controlled lists for signed offline analysis and untouched retriever lists for target-free replication. On the four controlled blocks, fully disjoint correlations are weak or heterogeneous (-0.061 to 0.281), whereas reusing both probe views yields 0.600 to 0.718; all paired contrasts are large (0.436 to 0.661) and Holm-significant. The overlap effect is positive in all four untouched-list blocks. Increasing the probe from two to four views clearly improves disjoint reliability in only one block. Moreover, the probe predicts aggregation-movement magnitude (Spearman rho = 0.142 to 0.426) but not stable signed target benefit, and 7 of 12 fixed-fraction probe-routing points are strictly dominated at measured cost. Thus, when the intended estimand is predictive information about unseen perturbation behavior, validation targets must be observation-disjoint from the probe to isolate that information; signed utility and cost-sensitive decisions remain separate questions.

</details>


### [254] [Discovery-Driven Integration of Disjoint Tables via Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26658)
*Md Ataur Rahman,Dimitris Sacharidis,Oscar Romero,Sergi Nadal*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出LOKI框架，将文本介导的连接路径发现问题形式化为细粒度任务，通过水平双向交叉注意力学习表格行与句子的上下文化表示，用全局表格-文本对比目标实现无局部监督的行-句子关联发现，并物化为带句子级溯源的类型化集成表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据湖中语义相关但缺少显式连接属性的异构表格难以集成。在Discovery-Driven Integration场景下，需先发现相关数据源及其缺失的关系结构；非结构化文本提供了连接互不相交表格的证据，但核心挑战在于发现能通过具体句子连接不同表格行的细粒度关系。

Method: 形式化任务为Text-Mediated Join Path Discovery。提出LOKI（Latent-space Optimization for Knowledge Integration）架构，采用水平双向交叉注意力，学习表格行与句子的上下文化表示；通过全局表格-文本对比目标，在无显式局部监督下涌现细粒度行-句子关联。将隐式关联转化为显式可解释的连接路径，组织成关系一致的组，并物化为带句子级溯源的类型化集成表。

Result: 在真实世界基准上，LOKI持续优于最先进的多模态数据发现方法；物化的类型化集成表达到0.982的宏类型对精度，且LLM API成本比直接提示低至多40倍。

Conclusion: LOKI通过对比学习隐式发现细粒度行-句子关联，优于仅检索粗粒度列-文本关联的现有方法，也不依赖预提供的行-文本链接、模式或查询，实现了可解释、类型化并可溯源的数据集成，兼顾精度与成本效率。

Abstract: Integrating heterogeneous datasets within data lakes is a critical challenge, particularly for semantically related tables that lack the explicit attributes needed to be joined. We study Discovery-Driven Integration, where the relevant sources and their missing relational structure must be discovered before integration. In this setting, unstructured text provides the evidence that connects otherwise disjoint tables. The fundamental challenge is to discover the relationships at a fine-grained level that connect individual rows from different tables through specific sentences. We formalize this task as Text-Mediated Join Path Discovery and propose a horizontal bidirectional cross-attention architecture called LOKI Latent-space Optimization for Knowledge Integration) that learns contextualized representations of table rows and sentences. Through a global table-text contrastive objective, fine-grained row-sentence associations emerge without explicit local supervision. Existing multi-modal discovery methods largely retrieve coarse-grained column-text associations, whereas integration systems assume supplied row-text links, schemas, or queries. LOKI instead transforms these implicit associations into explicit, interpretable join paths, organizes them into relation-consistent groups, and materializes them as typed integrated tables with sentence-level provenance. Comprehensive evaluations on real-world benchmarks demonstrate that LOKI consistently outperforms state-of-the-art multi-modal data discovery approaches, and materializes typed integrated tables with 0.982 macro typed-pair precision while being up to 40 times cheaper in LLM API cost than direct prompting.

</details>
